**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkBytes.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// Small byte helpers of the link (sub-project G2), shared by the frames, the pairing and the session. Foundation only.
// Hex on the wire and in the contract is lower-case: `isCanonicalHex` refuses a capital, and nothing here lower-cases a value to make it fit (docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md, "Notes for ports").

enum LinkBytes {
    static func hex(_ data: some Sequence<UInt8>) -> String {
        let digits = Array("0123456789abcdef".utf8)
        var out: [UInt8] = []
        for b in data { out.append(digits[Int(b >> 4)]); out.append(digits[Int(b & 15)]) }
        return String(decoding: out, as: UTF8.self)
    }

    /// The bytes of a hex string of either case, nil when it is not hex or has an odd length.
    static func data(hex: String) -> Data? {
        let u = Array(hex.utf8)
        guard u.count % 2 == 0 else { return nil }
        var out = Data(capacity: u.count / 2)
        func nibble(_ c: UInt8) -> UInt8? {
            switch c {
            case 48...57: return c - 48
            case 97...102: return c - 87
            case 65...70: return c - 55
            default: return nil
            }
        }
        var i = 0
        while i < u.count {
            guard let hi = nibble(u[i]), let lo = nibble(u[i + 1]) else { return nil }
            out.append(hi << 4 | lo)
            i += 2
        }
        return out
    }

    /// `bytes` bytes as exactly `2 * bytes` lower-case hex digits.
    static func isCanonicalHex(_ s: String?, bytes: Int) -> Bool {
        guard let s else { return false }
        let u = s.utf8
        return u.count == 2 * bytes && u.allSatisfy { ($0 >= 48 && $0 <= 57) || ($0 >= 97 && $0 <= 102) }
    }

    /// A 32-byte value (a nonce, a key, a commitment) as 64 lower-case hex digits.
    static func isHex32(_ s: String?) -> Bool { isCanonicalHex(s, bytes: 32) }
    /// A device id on the wire: 16 bytes, 32 lower-case hex digits.
    static func isIdHex(_ s: String?) -> Bool { isCanonicalHex(s, bytes: 16) }
    static let zeroId = String(repeating: "0", count: 32)

    static func beUInt64(_ v: UInt64) -> [UInt8] { (0..<8).map { UInt8((v >> UInt64(56 - 8 * $0)) & 0xFF) } }
    static func beUInt16(_ v: UInt16) -> [UInt8] { [UInt8(v >> 8), UInt8(v & 0xFF)] }
    static func readUInt64(_ b: ArraySlice<UInt8>) -> UInt64 { b.reduce(0) { $0 << 8 | UInt64($1) } }
    static func readUInt16(_ b: ArraySlice<UInt8>) -> UInt16 { b.reduce(0) { $0 << 8 | UInt16($1) } }
}

/// A machine's own device id as the pairing and the advert rules see it. `absent` is a machine that has no id yet (the unconfigured case); `null` is an id that is present and is
/// not an id, which the contract refuses fail-closed (a port never passes null: it omits the id when it has none).
enum LinkOwnId: Equatable, Sendable {
    case absent
    case null
    case value(String)
}
````

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkName.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// The name a received file is saved under (sub-project G2, docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md "File names"): `sanitize` gives the saved name or a reason, `uniqueName` the name to use when it is taken.
// A port of `sanitize_name` and `unique_name` of tests/fixtures/link/reference.py, replayed against filename-cases.json by scripts/test-link-name.sh. Foundation only.
//
// Everything here works on Unicode scalars, never on Characters: a Swift `Character` is a grapheme cluster and a Swift `String ==` is canonical equivalence, and neither is what the contract
// counts. The two Unicode tables that decide a name (`invisibleRanges` and `spaceRun`) are written out by hand, as in the reference, so they do not move with the system's Unicode version.
// The categories (Cc, Cf, Cs, Zl, Zp, Zs) and NFC/NFKC come from the system, which the contract allows and tells a port to re-check when the Unicode version changes.

enum LinkName {
    static let bytesMax = 200          // UTF-8 bytes of a saved name
    static let extBytesMax = 16        // the part after the last dot is an extension only up to this long
    static let collisionMax = 9999

    enum Result: Equatable, Sendable {
        case ok(String)
        case reject(String)    // "not-text", "invisible", "separator" or "empty"
    }

    /// Every Default_Ignorable code point (DerivedCoreProperties.txt) and U+2800, as in `INVISIBLE_RANGES`.
    static let invisibleRanges: [ClosedRange<UInt32>] = [
        0x00AD...0x00AD, 0x034F...0x034F, 0x061C...0x061C, 0x115F...0x1160, 0x17B4...0x17B5, 0x180B...0x180F, 0x200B...0x200F, 0x202A...0x202E,
        0x2060...0x206F, 0x2800...0x2800, 0x3164...0x3164, 0xFE00...0xFE0F, 0xFEFF...0xFEFF, 0xFFA0...0xFFA0, 0xFFF0...0xFFF8, 0x1BCA0...0x1BCA3,
        0x1D173...0x1D17A, 0xE0000...0xE0FFF,
    ]

    static func isInvisible(_ s: Unicode.Scalar) -> Bool { invisibleRanges.contains { $0.contains(s.value) } }

    /// Cc, Cf, Cs, Zl and Zp: controls, format characters (bidi controls, zero-width, BOM, soft hyphen), surrogates, line and paragraph separators.
    static func isBadCategory(_ s: Unicode.Scalar) -> Bool {
        switch s.properties.generalCategory {
        case .control, .format, .surrogate, .lineSeparator, .paragraphSeparator: return true
        default: return false
        }
    }

    static func isSpace(_ s: Unicode.Scalar) -> Bool { s == " " || s.properties.generalCategory == .spaceSeparator }

    /// `NAME_SPACE_RUN`: U+0009 to U+000D, U+0020, U+0085, U+00A0, U+1680, U+2000 to U+200A, U+2028, U+2029, U+202F, U+205F, U+3000.
    static func inSpaceRun(_ v: UInt32) -> Bool {
        (0x09...0x0D).contains(v) || v == 0x20 || v == 0x85 || v == 0xA0 || v == 0x1680 || (0x2000...0x200A).contains(v) || v == 0x2028 || v == 0x2029 || v == 0x202F || v == 0x205F || v == 0x3000
    }

    static func collapseSpaces(_ scalars: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> [Unicode.Scalar] {
        var out: [Unicode.Scalar] = []
        var inRun = false
        for s in scalars {
            if inSpaceRun(s.value) {
                if !inRun { out.append(" ") }
                inRun = true
            } else {
                out.append(s)
                inRun = false
            }
        }
        return out
    }

    static func scalars(_ s: String) -> [Unicode.Scalar] { Array(s.unicodeScalars) }
    static func string(_ s: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> String { var v = String.UnicodeScalarView(); v.append(contentsOf: s); return String(v) }
    static func byteCount(_ s: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> Int { s.reduce(0) { $0 + UTF8.width($1) } }
    static func nfc(_ s: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> [Unicode.Scalar] { scalars(string(s).precomposedStringWithCanonicalMapping) }
    static func nfkc(_ s: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> [Unicode.Scalar] { scalars(string(s).precomposedStringWithCompatibilityMapping) }

    /// Spaces off the start; spaces and dots off the end.
    static func trim(_ s: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> [Unicode.Scalar] {
        var a = 0, b = s.count
        while a < b, isSpace(s[a]) { a += 1 }
        while b > a, s[b - 1] == "." || isSpace(s[b - 1]) { b -= 1 }
        return Array(s[a..<b])
    }

    static let reserved: Set<[UInt8]> = {
        var names = ["CON", "PRN", "AUX", "NUL", "CONIN$", "CONOUT$", "COM\u{B9}", "COM\u{B2}", "COM\u{B3}", "LPT\u{B9}", "LPT\u{B2}", "LPT\u{B3}"]
        for n in 0...9 { names.append("COM\(n)"); names.append("LPT\(n)") }
        return Set(names.map { Array($0.utf8) })
    }()

    /// A Windows device name: the part before the first dot, spaces off its end, ASCII letters in capitals.
    static func isReserved(_ s: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> Bool {
        var head = Array(s.prefix { $0 != "." })
        while head.last == " " { head.removeLast() }
        let bytes = head.map { $0.value >= 97 && $0.value <= 122 ? Unicode.Scalar($0.value - 32)! : $0 }
        return reserved.contains(Array(string(bytes).utf8))
    }

    static func splitExt(_ name: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> (stem: [Unicode.Scalar], ext: [Unicode.Scalar]) {
        if let i = name.lastIndex(of: "."), i > 0, byteCount(Array(name[i...])) <= extBytesMax { return (Array(name[..<i]), Array(name[i...])) }
        return (name, [])
    }

    /// `name` cut to `bytesMax` bytes at a code point, keeping an extension of up to `extBytesMax` bytes; the spaces and dots the cut leaves at the end go.
    static func cut(_ name: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> [Unicode.Scalar] {
        if byteCount(name) <= bytesMax { return name }
        let (stem, ext) = splitExt(name)
        let room = bytesMax - byteCount(ext)
        var out: [Unicode.Scalar] = [], used = 0
        for s in stem {
            if used + UTF8.width(s) > room { break }
            out.append(s)
            used += UTF8.width(s)
        }
        return trim(out) + ext
    }

    static func sanitize(_ raw: String) -> Result {
        let nfcForm = collapseSpaces(nfc(scalars(raw))), nfkcForm = collapseSpaces(nfkc(scalars(raw)))
        if (nfcForm + nfkcForm).contains(where: { isBadCategory($0) || isInvisible($0) }) { return .reject("invisible") }
        if (nfcForm + nfkcForm).contains(where: { $0 == "/" || $0 == "\\" }) { return .reject("separator") }
        if trim(nfcForm).isEmpty || trim(nfkcForm).isEmpty { return .reject("empty") }
        let illegal = Set(Array("<>:\"|?*".unicodeScalars))
        func rewrite(_ s: [Unicode.Scalar]) -> [Unicode.Scalar] { trim(s.map { illegal.contains($0) ? "_" : $0 }) }
        var name = rewrite(nfcForm)
        let probe = rewrite(nfkcForm)
        if name.isEmpty { return .reject("empty") }
        if isReserved(name) || isReserved(probe) { name = ["_"] + name }
        name = cut(name)
        // The cut can leave a device name too ("CON" and spaces, the spaces cut away): the same test runs again on the cut name, NFC and NFKC.
        if isReserved(name) || isReserved(trim(nfkc(name))) { name = cut(["_"] + name) }
        if name.first == "." { name = cut(["_"] + name) }
        if trim(name).isEmpty || trim(nfkc(name)).isEmpty { return .reject("empty") }
        return .ok(string(name))
    }

    /// The comparison key of a name: NFC, case-folded (the usual volumes of both systems compare that way).
    static func key(_ name: String) -> String { name.precomposedStringWithCanonicalMapping.folding(options: .caseInsensitive, locale: nil) }

    /// `name` itself when no taken name equals it, else `stem (n)ext` for the smallest n from 1 that is free; nil after 9999 tries. The caller still creates the file exclusively
    /// (`O_EXCL`) and asks again on "already exists": the volume decides which names are the same file.
    static func uniqueName(_ name: String, taken: [String]) -> String? {
        let used = Set(taken.map(key))
        if !used.contains(key(name)) { return name }
        let (stem, ext) = splitExt(scalars(name))
        for n in 1...collisionMax {
            let cand = "\(string(stem)) (\(n))\(string(ext))"
            if !used.contains(key(cand)) { return cand }
        }
        return nil
    }
}
````

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkFrames.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// The bytes of the link on the wire (sub-project G2, docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md "On the wire"): records, the preamble, the hello, the advert's TXT record and the frames. A port of
// `encode_record`, `split_records`, `encode_preamble`/`decode_preamble`, `encode_hello`/`decode_hello`, `parse_txt`, `encode_frame`/`decode_frame` of tests/fixtures/link/reference.py,
// replayed against frames.json by scripts/test-link-frames.sh. Foundation only. Every decoder is strict: a name is UTF-8 without surrogates or overlong forms (`String(validating:)`, never
// a decoder that repairs), `kind` is printable ASCII, nothing may be left over, and a bad input is an error with a cause, never a trap.

enum LinkFrameError: String, Error, Equatable, Sendable {
    case empty, truncated, trailing, reason, kind, utf8, length, version, mode, id
    case unknownType = "unknown-type"
    case chunkEmpty = "chunk-empty"
    case chunkLong = "chunk-long"
    case nameLength = "name-length"
    case nameChars = "name-chars"
    case deviceId = "device-id"
    case ownId = "own-id"
    case recordLength = "record-length"
    case recordEmpty = "record-empty"
    case notARecord = "not-a-record"
}

enum LinkWire {
    static let protocolVersion: UInt8 = 1
    static let idLen = 16
    static let noiseMax = 65535
    static let tag = 16
    static let chunkMax = noiseMax - tag - 1 - idLen - 8   // 65494: a chunk frame and the tag fill one Noise message exactly
    static let chunkSize = 32768                            // what a sender cuts a file into
    static let rawNameMax = 1020                            // bytes of a name on the wire: 255 code points of up to 4 bytes
    static let kindMax = 127
    static let deviceNameMax = 63
    static let sizeMax: UInt64 = 4 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024    // 4 GiB inclusive
    static let declineBadOffer: UInt8 = 4
    static let typePairYes: UInt8 = 0x21, typePairNo: UInt8 = 0x22

    // MARK: Records

    /// A record is a u16 big-endian length (1 to 65535) and that many bytes. Every Noise message, handshake or transport, travels in one.
    static func encodeRecord(_ payload: Data) throws -> Data {
        guard (1...noiseMax).contains(payload.count) else { throw LinkFrameError.recordLength }
        return Data(LinkBytes.beUInt16(UInt16(payload.count))) + payload
    }

    /// The whole records at the start of `buffer` and the bytes of a record that has not arrived completely. A length of 0 is an error and closes the connection.
    static func splitRecords(_ buffer: Data) throws -> (records: [Data], rest: Data) {
        let b = [UInt8](buffer)
        var out: [Data] = [], pos = 0
        while b.count - pos >= 2 {
            let n = Int(LinkBytes.readUInt16(b[pos..<pos + 2]))
            if n == 0 { throw LinkFrameError.recordEmpty }
            if b.count - pos - 2 < n { break }
            out.append(Data(b[pos + 2..<pos + 2 + n]))
            pos += 2 + n
        }
        return (out, Data(b[pos...]))
    }

    // MARK: Preamble

    enum Mode: UInt8, Sendable { case pair = 1, transfer = 2 }
    struct Preamble: Equatable, Sendable { let mode: Mode; let id: String }

    /// The first record of a connection, in the clear: version, mode and the sender's device id (16 zero bytes when pairing).
    static func encodePreamble(mode: Mode, deviceId: Data) -> Data { Data([protocolVersion, mode.rawValue]) + deviceId }

    static func decodePreamble(_ data: Data) throws -> Preamble {
        let b = [UInt8](data)
        guard b.count == 2 + idLen else { throw LinkFrameError.length }
        guard b[0] == protocolVersion else { throw LinkFrameError.version }
        guard let mode = Mode(rawValue: b[1]) else { throw LinkFrameError.mode }
        let zero = b[2...].allSatisfy { $0 == 0 }
        guard (mode == .pair) == zero else { throw LinkFrameError.deviceId }
        return Preamble(mode: mode, id: LinkBytes.hex(b[2...]))
    }

    // MARK: Device name, hello, advert

    /// A device name is shown on a card: 1 to 63 bytes of strict UTF-8, no control, format or separator character, no invisible character (the table of a file name), no space at either end.
    static func deviceName(_ raw: [UInt8]) throws -> String {
        guard (1...deviceNameMax).contains(raw.count) else { throw LinkFrameError.nameLength }
        guard let s = String(validating: raw, as: UTF8.self) else { throw LinkFrameError.utf8 }
        let sc = Array(s.unicodeScalars)
        // Python's strip() also removes the other white space, but every such character is a control, a separator or a Zs, and the first two are refused above.
        if sc.contains(where: { LinkName.isBadCategory($0) || LinkName.isInvisible($0) }) || LinkName.isSpace(sc[0]) || LinkName.isSpace(sc[sc.count - 1]) { throw LinkFrameError.nameChars }
        return s
    }

    struct Hello: Equatable, Sendable { let id: String; let nonce: String; let name: String }

    /// The payload of the second and third pairing messages: version, device id, a 32-byte nonce, name length and name. It is encrypted, so the name on the card is the authenticated one.
    static func encodeHello(deviceId: Data, nonce: Data, name: String) -> Data {
        let nb = Array(name.utf8)
        return Data([protocolVersion]) + deviceId + nonce + Data([UInt8(nb.count)]) + Data(nb)
    }

    static func decodeHello(_ data: Data) throws -> Hello {
        let b = [UInt8](data)
        guard b.count >= 2 + idLen + 32 else { throw LinkFrameError.truncated }
        guard b[0] == protocolVersion else { throw LinkFrameError.version }
        guard !b[1..<1 + idLen].allSatisfy({ $0 == 0 }) else { throw LinkFrameError.deviceId }
        let n = Int(b[1 + idLen + 32]), body = Array(b[(2 + idLen + 32)...])
        guard body.count >= n else { throw LinkFrameError.truncated }
        guard body.count == n else { throw LinkFrameError.trailing }
        return Hello(id: LinkBytes.hex(b[1..<1 + idLen]), nonce: LinkBytes.hex(b[1 + idLen..<1 + idLen + 32]), name: try deviceName(body))
    }

    struct Advert: Equatable, Sendable { let id: String; let name: String }

    /// An advert's TXT record (`_notchmeow._tcp`): anyone on the network can send one, so it is checked like a name on a card. `v` must be the text "1", `id` 32 lower-case hex digits that are not
    /// all zero. An advert with this machine's own id is refused (`own-id`); an own id that is not 32 lower-case hex digits, a null one included, refuses every advert (`device-id`, fail closed).
    /// It carries no key: a paired id under another key is decided at the hello.
    static func parseTxt(_ txt: [String: String], ownId: LinkOwnId = .absent) throws -> Advert {
        guard txt["v"] == String(protocolVersion) else { throw LinkFrameError.version }
        guard let id = txt["id"], LinkBytes.isIdHex(id), id != LinkBytes.zeroId else { throw LinkFrameError.deviceId }
        switch ownId {
        case .absent: break
        case .null: throw LinkFrameError.deviceId
        case .value(let own):
            guard LinkBytes.isIdHex(own) else { throw LinkFrameError.deviceId }
            if id == own { throw LinkFrameError.ownId }
        }
        guard let name = txt["name"] else { throw LinkFrameError.nameLength }
        return Advert(id: id, name: try deviceName(Array(name.utf8)))
    }

    // MARK: Frames

    enum Frame: Equatable, Sendable {
        case offer(id: Data, size: UInt64, name: String, kind: String)
        case accept(id: Data)
        case decline(id: Data, reason: UInt8)
        case chunk(id: Data, offset: UInt64, data: Data)
        case done(id: Data)
        case cancel(id: Data)
        case pairYes
        case pairNo
    }

    /// A frame is the plaintext of one Noise transport message: a type byte, the 16-byte transfer id, then the body (numbers big-endian). The two pairing answers are the type byte alone.
    static func encode(_ frame: Frame) throws -> Data {
        func header(_ type: UInt8, _ id: Data) throws -> [UInt8] {
            guard id.count == idLen else { throw LinkFrameError.id }
            return [type] + [UInt8](id)
        }
        switch frame {
        case .pairYes: return Data([typePairYes])
        case .pairNo: return Data([typePairNo])
        case .offer(let id, let size, let name, let kind):
            let nb = Array(name.utf8), kb = Array(kind.utf8)
            return Data(try header(1, id) + LinkBytes.beUInt64(size) + LinkBytes.beUInt16(UInt16(truncatingIfNeeded: nb.count)) + nb + [UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: kb.count)] + kb)
        case .accept(let id): return Data(try header(2, id))
        case .decline(let id, let reason): return Data(try header(3, id) + [reason])
        case .chunk(let id, let offset, let data): return Data(try header(4, id) + LinkBytes.beUInt64(offset)) + data
        case .done(let id): return Data(try header(5, id))
        case .cancel(let id): return Data(try header(6, id))
        }
    }

    static func decode(_ data: Data) throws -> Frame {
        let b = [UInt8](data)
        guard let type = b.first else { throw LinkFrameError.empty }
        guard (1...6).contains(type) || type == typePairYes || type == typePairNo else { throw LinkFrameError.unknownType }
        let body = Array(b.dropFirst())
        if type == typePairYes || type == typePairNo {
            guard body.isEmpty else { throw LinkFrameError.trailing }
            return type == typePairYes ? .pairYes : .pairNo
        }
        guard body.count >= idLen else { throw LinkFrameError.truncated }
        let id = Data(body[..<idLen]), rest = Array(body[idLen...])
        switch type {
        case 2, 5, 6:
            guard rest.isEmpty else { throw LinkFrameError.trailing }
            return type == 2 ? .accept(id: id) : type == 5 ? .done(id: id) : .cancel(id: id)
        case 3:
            guard rest.count >= 1 else { throw LinkFrameError.truncated }
            guard rest.count == 1 else { throw LinkFrameError.trailing }
            guard rest[0] <= declineBadOffer else { throw LinkFrameError.reason }
            return .decline(id: id, reason: rest[0])
        case 4:
            guard rest.count >= 8 else { throw LinkFrameError.truncated }
            let payload = Array(rest[8...])
            guard !payload.isEmpty else { throw LinkFrameError.chunkEmpty }
            guard payload.count <= chunkMax else { throw LinkFrameError.chunkLong }
            return .chunk(id: id, offset: LinkBytes.readUInt64(rest[0..<8]), data: Data(payload))
        default:
            guard rest.count >= 10 else { throw LinkFrameError.truncated }
            let size = LinkBytes.readUInt64(rest[0..<8]), n = Int(LinkBytes.readUInt16(rest[8..<10]))
            guard (1...rawNameMax).contains(n) else { throw LinkFrameError.nameLength }
            guard rest.count >= 10 + n + 1 else { throw LinkFrameError.truncated }
            let nameBytes = Array(rest[10..<10 + n]), k = Int(rest[10 + n]), kindBytes = Array(rest[(11 + n)...])
            guard kindBytes.count >= k else { throw LinkFrameError.truncated }
            guard kindBytes.count == k else { throw LinkFrameError.trailing }
            guard k <= kindMax, kindBytes.allSatisfy({ (0x20...0x7E).contains($0) }) else { throw LinkFrameError.kind }
            guard let name = String(validating: nameBytes, as: UTF8.self) else { throw LinkFrameError.utf8 }
            return .offer(id: id, size: size, name: name, kind: String(decoding: kindBytes, as: UTF8.self))
        }
    }

    /// The pieces a sender cuts a file of `size` bytes into: (offset, length) pairs of `chunkSize` bytes, the last one short. An empty file has none.
    static func planChunks(size: UInt64, chunkSize: Int = chunkSize) -> [(offset: UInt64, length: Int)] {
        guard size > 0, chunkSize > 0 else { return [] }
        var out: [(UInt64, Int)] = [], at: UInt64 = 0
        while at < size { let n = Int(min(UInt64(chunkSize), size - at)); out.append((at, n)); at += UInt64(n) }
        return out
    }
}
````

### Task 3: The pairing and the transfer rules: the machines and the clocks

**Agent:** `nm-implementer-light` (Haiku 5.5, medium). The code is given in full; the fixtures prove it.

**Files:**
- Create: `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkPairing.swift`, `LinkFlow.swift`; `tests/LinkPairingTests.swift`, `tests/LinkFlowTests.swift`; `scripts/test-link-pairing.sh`, `scripts/test-link-flow.sh`
- Modify: `NotchBuddy/NotchBuddy.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` (regenerated)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pairing-cases.json` (`digits`, `commitLabel`, `codeLabel`, `gapMs`, `windowMs`, `offerTimeoutMs`, `commits[]`, `codes[]`, `invalid[]`, `session`, `gapCases[]`, `windowCases[]`, `offerCases[]`, `responder[]`, `initiator[]`, `machineTrips[]`), `flow-cases.json` (`receiver[]`, `sender[]`, `trips[]`, `chunkPlans[]`, `aborts`), `LinkBytes`, `LinkName`, `LinkWire` (Task 2), `noise-vectors.json` for the `session` row.
- Produces: `LinkPairing` (`commitOf`, `code`, `attemptAllowed`, `windowOpen`, `offerExpired`, **`Gate`** (`devicesAppeared()`, `devicesDisappeared()`, `pairClicked(now:)`, `allows(now:)`), `Refusal`, `Notify`, `Send`, `Ids`, `Out<State>`, `peerRefusal`, `ResponderState`/`ResponderEvent`/`responderStep`, `InitiatorState`/`InitiatorEvent`/`initiatorStep`), `LinkFlow` (`Notify`, `Abort`, `Decline`, `Out<State>`, `ReceiverState`, `Event`, `receiverStep`, `SenderState`, `SenderEvent`, `senderStep`).

**The gate (the owner's rule of 2026-10-15; not in the contract, so not in the fixtures).** `LinkPairing.Gate` says whether an incoming pairing may run: only while Settings → Devices is open on this Mac (a counter of openers), or within 2 minutes after the user clicked "Pair a machine" here. The window is the contract's own `windowOpen`, so it is open at the click, open 1 ms before 2 minutes and **closed at exactly 120 s**, and a clock that went back or a missing clock refuses. `LinkPairingTests.swift` pins these edges beside the fixtures.

**How the typed port meets the JSON fixtures.** The machines take typed state and events; the test bridges the fixtures' JSON into them and the answers back (a clock that is text or a boolean becomes `nil`, a `last` that is given but is not whole milliseconds becomes -1, which the rule refuses, an answer that is not exactly `"yes"` is a no, an `own_id` of null is `LinkOwnId.null`). Reading the fixture rows, the port is checked row by row and as whole pairings and whole transfers.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests and their scripts**

**Create `tests/LinkPairingTests.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// Pairing (docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md "The pairing code"): pairing-cases.json replayed against LinkPairing.swift: the commitment, the code, the clocks and the two machines row by row.
// The fixtures hold state and events as JSON; the bridge below turns them into the typed values the port takes and the machines' answers back into JSON.
// Run it through scripts/test-link-pairing.sh.

@main
enum LinkPairingTests {
    typealias P = LinkPairing

    // MARK: JSON bridge

    /// A whole number of milliseconds, or nil (text, a boolean, null, a fraction).
    static func msInt(_ v: Any?) -> Int? {
        guard let n = v as? NSNumber, CFGetTypeID(n) != CFBooleanGetTypeID(), CFNumberIsFloatType(n) == false else { return nil }
        return n.intValue
    }
    /// The time of the last attempt: null is "none yet"; a value that is given but is not whole milliseconds counts as -1, which the rule refuses (the typed port has no way to hold text).
    static func lastInt(_ v: Any?) -> Int? { (v == nil || v is NSNull) ? nil : (msInt(v) ?? -1) }
    static func str(_ v: Any?) -> String? { v as? String }
    static func isYes(_ v: Any?) -> Bool { (v as? String) == "yes" }

    static func ids(_ s: [String: Any]) -> P.Ids {
        var i = P.Ids()
        if s.keys.contains("own_id") { i.ownId = (s["own_id"] as? String).map { .value($0) } ?? .null }
        if let p = s["paired"] as? [String: String] { i.paired = p }
        return i
    }
    static func idsJSON(_ i: P.Ids) -> [String: Any] {
        var o: [String: Any] = [:]
        switch i.ownId { case .absent: break; case .null: o["own_id"] = NSNull(); case .value(let v): o["own_id"] = v }
        if let p = i.paired { o["paired"] = p }
        return o
    }
    static func yes(_ b: Bool) -> Any { b ? "yes" : NSNull() }

    static func responderState(_ s: [String: Any]) -> P.ResponderState {
        var st = P.ResponderState(seen: (s["seen"] as! [[String: Any]]).map { P.Seen(commit: $0["commit"] as! String, at: $0["at"] as! Int) }, last: msInt(s["last"]), ids: ids(s))
        switch s["s"] as! String {
        case "committed": st.phase = .committed(commit: s["commit"] as! String, start: s["start"] as! Int, nonce: s["nonce"] as! String)
        case "coded": st.phase = .coded(start: s["start"] as! Int, code: s["code"] as! String, mine: isYes(s["mine"]), theirs: isYes(s["theirs"]))
        default: break
        }
        return st
    }
    static func json(_ st: P.ResponderState) -> [String: Any] {
        var o = idsJSON(st.ids)
        o["seen"] = st.seen.map { ["commit": $0.commit, "at": $0.at] as [String: Any] }
        o["last"] = st.last.map { $0 as Any } ?? NSNull()
        switch st.phase {
        case .idle: o["s"] = "idle"
        case .committed(let c, let start, let nonce): o["s"] = "committed"; o["commit"] = c; o["start"] = start; o["nonce"] = nonce
        case .coded(let start, let code, let mine, let theirs): o["s"] = "coded"; o["start"] = start; o["code"] = code; o["mine"] = yes(mine); o["theirs"] = yes(theirs)
        }
        return o
    }
    static func responderEvent(_ e: [String: Any]) -> P.ResponderEvent {
        let now = msInt(e["now"])
        if e.keys.contains("commit") { return .commit(commit: str(e["commit"]), nonce: str(e["nonce"]), now: now) }
        if let r = e["reveal"] as? [String: Any] { return .reveal(P.Reveal(nonce: str(r["nonce"]), staticKey: str(r["static"]), own: str(r["own"]), peerId: str(r["peerId"])), now: now) }
        if e.keys.contains("user") { return .user(yes: isYes(e["user"]), now: now) }
        if e.keys.contains("peer") { return .peer(yes: isYes(e["peer"]), now: now) }
        return .unknown
    }

    static func initiatorState(_ s: [String: Any]) -> P.InitiatorState {
        var st = P.InitiatorState(last: msInt(s["last"]), ids: ids(s))
        switch s["s"] as! String {
        case "started": st.phase = .started(nonce: s["nonce"] as! String, staticKey: s["static"] as! String, start: s["start"] as! Int)
        case "coded": st.phase = .coded(start: s["start"] as! Int, code: s["code"] as! String, mine: isYes(s["mine"]), theirs: isYes(s["theirs"]))
        default: break
        }
        return st
    }
    static func json(_ st: P.InitiatorState) -> [String: Any] {
        var o = idsJSON(st.ids)
        o["last"] = st.last.map { $0 as Any } ?? NSNull()
        switch st.phase {
        case .idle: o["s"] = "idle"
        case .started(let n, let s, let start): o["s"] = "started"; o["nonce"] = n; o["static"] = s; o["start"] = start
        case .coded(let start, let code, let mine, let theirs): o["s"] = "coded"; o["start"] = start; o["code"] = code; o["mine"] = yes(mine); o["theirs"] = yes(theirs)
        }
        return o
    }
    static func initiatorEvent(_ e: [String: Any]) -> P.InitiatorEvent {
        let now = msInt(e["now"])
        if let s = e["start"] as? [String: Any] { return .start(nonce: str(s["nonce"]), staticKey: str(s["static"]), now: now) }
        if e.keys.contains("nonce") { return .nonce(str(e["nonce"]), peerStatic: str(e["peerStatic"]), peerId: str(e["peerId"]), now: now) }
        if e.keys.contains("user") { return .user(yes: isYes(e["user"]), now: now) }
        if e.keys.contains("peer") { return .peer(yes: isYes(e["peer"]), now: now) }
        return .unknown
    }

    static func outJSON<S: Equatable & Sendable>(_ o: P.Out<S>, _ state: [String: Any]) -> [String: Any] {
        let send: [[String: Any]] = o.send.map {
            switch $0 {
            case .nonce(let n): return ["nonce": n]
            case .commit(let c): return ["commit": c]
            case .reveal(let r): return ["reveal": r]
            case .pair(let y): return ["pair": y ? "yes" : "no"]
            }
        }
        return ["state": state, "send": send, "notify": o.notify?.rawValue ?? NSNull(), "because": o.because?.rawValue ?? NSNull()]
    }

    static func responderRun(_ state: [String: Any], _ event: [String: Any]) -> ([String: Any], [String: Any]) {
        let o = P.responderStep(responderState(state), responderEvent(event))
        return (outJSON(o, json(o.state)), json(o.state))
    }
    static func initiatorRun(_ state: [String: Any], _ event: [String: Any]) -> ([String: Any], [String: Any]) {
        let o = P.initiatorStep(initiatorState(state), initiatorEvent(event))
        return (outJSON(o, json(o.state)), json(o.state))
    }

    // MARK: Tests

    @MainActor static func main() {
        let f = CT.fixture("pairing-cases.json", dir: "link")
        func data(_ s: String) -> Data { LinkBytes.data(hex: s)! }

        CT.section("the constants")
        CT.check("6 digits, 30 s, 2 minutes, 2 minutes", [f["digits"] as! Int, f["gapMs"] as! Int, f["windowMs"] as! Int, f["offerTimeoutMs"] as! Int], [P.codeDigits, P.gapMs, P.windowMs, P.offerTimeoutMs])
        CT.check("the labels", [String(decoding: P.commitLabel, as: UTF8.self), String(decoding: P.codeLabel, as: UTF8.self)], [f["commitLabel"] as! String, f["codeLabel"] as! String])

        CT.section("commits, codes, invalid")
        var diffs: [String] = []
        for c in f["commits"] as! [[String: Any]] where P.commitOf(nonce: data(c["nonce"] as! String), staticKey: data(c["static"] as! String)).map(LinkBytes.hex) != (c["commit"] as! String) { diffs.append(c["name"] as! String) }
        CT.check("every commitment", diffs, [])
        diffs = []
        for c in f["codes"] as! [[String: Any]] where P.code(nonceI: data(c["nonceI"] as! String), nonceR: data(c["nonceR"] as! String), staticI: data(c["staticI"] as! String), staticR: data(c["staticR"] as! String)) != (c["code"] as! String) { diffs.append(c["name"] as! String) }
        CT.check("every code, leading zeros kept", diffs, [])
        diffs = []
        for c in f["invalid"] as! [[String: Any]] {
            var v: [String: Data] = ["nonceI": data("a1" + String(repeating: "a1", count: 31)), "nonceR": data("b1" + String(repeating: "b1", count: 31)), "staticI": data("c1" + String(repeating: "c1", count: 31)), "staticR": data("c2" + String(repeating: "c2", count: 31))]
            // The field is text that is not 32 bytes, not hex or missing: as bytes it is what hex decoding gives, or nothing (then the field has no 32 bytes either).
            v[c["field"] as! String] = (c["value"] as? String).flatMap { LinkBytes.data(hex: $0) } ?? Data()
            if P.code(nonceI: v["nonceI"]!, nonceR: v["nonceR"]!, staticI: v["staticI"]!, staticR: v["staticR"]!) != nil { diffs.append(c["name"] as! String) }
        }
        CT.check("a nonce or a key that is not 32 bytes gives no code", diffs, [])
        CT.checkTrue("commit_of refuses a short nonce", P.commitOf(nonce: Data(count: 31), staticKey: Data(count: 32)) == nil)

        CT.section("the pairing inside the xx-link Noise vector")
        let s = f["session"] as! [String: Any]
        let vec = ((CT.fixture("noise-vectors.json", dir: "link")["vectors"] as! [[String: Any]]).first { ($0["id"] as! String) == (s["vector"] as! String) })!
        let payloads = (vec["messages"] as! [[String: String]]).prefix(3).map { data($0["payload"]!) }
        let hr = try! LinkWire.decodeHello(payloads[1]), hi = try! LinkWire.decodeHello(payloads[2])
        CT.check("the first payload is the commitment and the hellos carry the nonces the case names", [LinkBytes.hex(payloads[0]), hi.nonce, hr.nonce], [s["commit"] as! String, s["nonceI"] as! String, s["nonceR"] as! String])
        CT.check("the code of that pairing is the one the case names", P.code(nonceI: data(hi.nonce), nonceR: data(hr.nonce), staticI: data(s["staticI"] as! String), staticR: data(s["staticR"] as! String)), (s["code"] as! String))

        CT.section("the clocks")
        diffs = []
        for c in f["gapCases"] as! [[String: Any]] where P.attemptAllowed(last: lastInt(c["last"]), now: msInt(c["now"])) != (c["out"] as! Bool) { diffs.append(c["name"] as! String) }
        for c in f["windowCases"] as! [[String: Any]] where P.windowOpen(start: msInt(c["start"]), now: msInt(c["now"])) != (c["out"] as! Bool) { diffs.append(c["name"] as! String) }
        for c in f["offerCases"] as! [[String: Any]] where P.offerExpired(offered: msInt(c["offered"]), now: msInt(c["now"])) != (c["out"] as! Bool) { diffs.append(c["name"] as! String) }
        CT.check("the attempt gap, the pairing window and the offer timeout, boundaries and bad values", diffs, [])

        CT.section("who may start a pairing with this Mac (the owner's rule, 2026-10-15)")
        var gate = P.Gate()
        CT.check("a fresh Mac refuses an incoming pairing", gate.allows(now: 1000), false)
        gate.devicesAppeared()
        CT.check("while Settings, Devices is open it allows one", gate.allows(now: 1000), true)
        gate.devicesAppeared(); gate.devicesDisappeared()
        CT.check("two openers and one closer: still open; the last closer shuts it", [gate.allows(now: 1000), { var g = gate; g.devicesDisappeared(); g.devicesDisappeared(); return g.allows(now: 1000) }()], [true, false])
        var clicked = P.Gate()
        clicked.pairClicked(now: 5000)
        CT.check("after a click on Pair here the window is the contract's: open at the click, 1 ms before 2 minutes, closed at exactly 120 s, closed long after",
                 [clicked.allows(now: 5000), clicked.allows(now: 124_999), clicked.allows(now: 125_000), clicked.allows(now: 900_000)], [true, true, false, false])
        CT.check("a clock that went back, a missing clock and a gate never clicked all refuse", [clicked.allows(now: 4_999), clicked.allows(now: nil), P.Gate().allows(now: 5000)], [false, false, false])
        var both = clicked; both.devicesAppeared()
        CT.check("Devices open keeps it open past the 2 minutes", both.allows(now: 900_000), true)
        CT.check("a later click opens a new window", { var g = clicked; g.pairClicked(now: 200_000); return [g.allows(now: 125_000), g.allows(now: 319_999), g.allows(now: 320_000)] }(), [false, true, false])

        CT.section("the responder, row by row")
        diffs = []
        let responder = f["responder"] as! [[String: Any]]
        for r in responder {
            let (got, _) = responderRun(r["state"] as! [String: Any], r["event"] as! [String: Any])
            let d = CT.diffs(got, r["out"] as! [String: Any])
            if !d.isEmpty { diffs.append("\(r["name"] as! String): \(d.prefix(2))") }
        }
        CT.check("all \(responder.count) rows", diffs, [])
        CT.section("the initiator, row by row")
        diffs = []
        let initiator = f["initiator"] as! [[String: Any]]
        for r in initiator {
            let (got, _) = initiatorRun(r["state"] as! [String: Any], r["event"] as! [String: Any])
            let d = CT.diffs(got, r["out"] as! [String: Any])
            if !d.isEmpty { diffs.append("\(r["name"] as! String): \(d.prefix(2))") }
        }
        CT.check("all \(initiator.count) rows", diffs, [])

        CT.section("whole pairings")
        diffs = []
        for t in f["machineTrips"] as! [[String: Any]] {
            var state = t["from"] as! [String: Any]
            let events = t["events"] as! [[String: Any]], outs = t["out"] as! [[String: Any]]
            for (i, e) in events.enumerated() {
                let (got, next) = (t["side"] as! String) == "responder" ? responderRun(state, e) : initiatorRun(state, e)
                state = next
                let d = CT.diffs(got, outs[i])
                if !d.isEmpty { diffs.append("\(t["name"] as! String) step \(i): \(d.prefix(2))") }
            }
        }
        CT.check("all trips, step by step", diffs, [])
        CT.finish()
    }
}
````

**Create `tests/LinkFlowTests.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// What each side does with each frame (docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md): flow-cases.json replayed against LinkFlow.swift, whole transfers event by event, the chunk plans, and a seeded fuzz of random event scripts.
// Run it through scripts/test-link-flow.sh.

@main
enum LinkFlowTests {
    typealias W = LinkWire

    static func bytes(_ hex: String) -> Data { LinkBytes.data(hex: hex)! }
    static func u64(_ v: Any?) -> UInt64 { (v as! NSNumber).uint64Value }

    /// A fixture frame (a dictionary) as the typed frame; and back.
    static func frame(_ d: [String: Any]) -> W.Frame {
        switch d["type"] as! String {
        case "pair-yes": return .pairYes
        case "pair-no": return .pairNo
        case "offer": return .offer(id: bytes(d["id"] as! String), size: u64(d["size"]), name: d["name"] as! String, kind: d["kind"] as! String)
        case "accept": return .accept(id: bytes(d["id"] as! String))
        case "decline": return .decline(id: bytes(d["id"] as! String), reason: UInt8(d["reason"] as! Int))
        case "chunk": return .chunk(id: bytes(d["id"] as! String), offset: u64(d["offset"]), data: bytes(d["data"] as! String))
        case "done": return .done(id: bytes(d["id"] as! String))
        default: return .cancel(id: bytes(d["id"] as! String))
        }
    }
    static func json(_ f: W.Frame) -> [String: Any] {
        switch f {
        case .pairYes: return ["type": "pair-yes"]
        case .pairNo: return ["type": "pair-no"]
        case .offer(let id, let size, let name, let kind): return ["type": "offer", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id), "size": NSNumber(value: size), "name": name, "kind": kind]
        case .accept(let id): return ["type": "accept", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id)]
        case .decline(let id, let r): return ["type": "decline", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id), "reason": Int(r)]
        case .chunk(let id, let o, let d): return ["type": "chunk", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id), "offset": NSNumber(value: o), "data": LinkBytes.hex(d)]
        case .done(let id): return ["type": "done", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id)]
        case .cancel(let id): return ["type": "cancel", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id)]
        }
    }

    static func receiverState(_ s: [String: Any]) -> LinkFlow.ReceiverState {
        let id = (s["id"] as? String).map(bytes) ?? Data()
        switch s["s"] as! String {
        case "offered": return .offered(id: id, size: (s["size"] as? NSNumber)?.uint64Value ?? 0, name: (s["name"] as? String) ?? "")
        case "receiving": return .receiving(id: id, size: u64(s["size"]), got: u64(s["got"]))
        default: return .idle
        }
    }
    static func json(_ s: LinkFlow.ReceiverState) -> [String: Any] {
        switch s {
        case .idle: return ["s": "idle"]
        case .offered(let id, let size, let name): return ["s": "offered", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id), "size": NSNumber(value: size), "name": name]
        case .receiving(let id, let size, let got): return ["s": "receiving", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id), "size": NSNumber(value: size), "got": NSNumber(value: got)]
        }
    }
    static func senderState(_ s: [String: Any]) -> LinkFlow.SenderState {
        let id = (s["id"] as? String).map(bytes) ?? Data()
        switch s["s"] as! String { case "offered": return .offered(id: id); case "sending": return .sending(id: id); default: return .idle }
    }
    static func json(_ s: LinkFlow.SenderState) -> [String: Any] {
        switch s {
        case .idle: return ["s": "idle"]
        case .offered(let id): return ["s": "offered", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id)]
        case .sending(let id): return ["s": "sending", "id": LinkBytes.hex(id)]
        }
    }
    static func receiverEvent(_ e: [String: Any]) -> LinkFlow.Event {
        if let u = e["user"] as? String { return .user(u == "accept" ? .accept : u == "decline" ? .decline : .cancel) }
        if e["timeout"] as? Bool == true { return .timeout }
        return .frame(frame(e["frame"] as! [String: Any]))
    }
    static func senderEvent(_ e: [String: Any]) -> LinkFlow.SenderEvent {
        if let u = e["user"] as? String { return u == "cancel" ? .userCancel : .userOther }
        return .frame(frame(e["frame"] as! [String: Any]))
    }
    static func outJSON<S: Equatable & Sendable>(_ o: LinkFlow.Out<S>, _ state: [String: Any]) -> [String: Any] {
        ["state": state, "send": o.send.map(json), "notify": o.notify?.rawValue ?? NSNull(), "abort": o.abort?.rawValue ?? NSNull()]
    }
    static func runReceiver(_ s: [String: Any], _ e: [String: Any]) -> ([String: Any], [String: Any]) {
        let o = LinkFlow.receiverStep(receiverState(s), receiverEvent(e)); return (outJSON(o, json(o.state)), json(o.state))
    }
    static func runSender(_ s: [String: Any], _ e: [String: Any]) -> ([String: Any], [String: Any]) {
        let o = LinkFlow.senderStep(senderState(s), senderEvent(e)); return (outJSON(o, json(o.state)), json(o.state))
    }

    struct Lcg { var s: UInt64; mutating func next(_ n: Int) -> Int { s = s &* 6364136223846793005 &+ 1442695040888963407; return Int((s >> 33) % UInt64(n)) } }

    @MainActor static func main() {
        let f = CT.fixture("flow-cases.json", dir: "link")
        CT.section("the constants")
        CT.check("chunk size and 4 GiB", [Int(truncating: f["chunkSize"] as! NSNumber), Int(u64(f["sizeMax"]))], [W.chunkSize, Int(W.sizeMax)])
        CT.check("the abort reasons", f["aborts"] as! [String], ["unexpected", "id", "order", "overflow", "short"])

        CT.section("the receiver and the sender, row by row")
        var diffs: [String] = []
        let receiver = f["receiver"] as! [[String: Any]], sender = f["sender"] as! [[String: Any]]
        for c in receiver { let d = CT.diffs(runReceiver(c["state"] as! [String: Any], c["event"] as! [String: Any]).0, c["out"] as! [String: Any]); if !d.isEmpty { diffs.append("receiver: \(c["name"] as! String): \(d.prefix(2))") } }
        for c in sender { let d = CT.diffs(runSender(c["state"] as! [String: Any], c["event"] as! [String: Any]).0, c["out"] as! [String: Any]); if !d.isEmpty { diffs.append("sender: \(c["name"] as! String): \(d.prefix(2))") } }
        CT.check("all \(receiver.count) receiver rows and \(sender.count) sender rows", diffs, [])

        CT.section("whole transfers")
        diffs = []
        for t in f["trips"] as! [[String: Any]] {
            var state = t["from"] as! [String: Any]
            let outs = t["out"] as! [[String: Any]]
            for (i, e) in (t["events"] as! [[String: Any]]).enumerated() {
                let (got, next) = (t["side"] as! String) == "receiver" ? runReceiver(state, e) : runSender(state, e)
                state = next
                let d = CT.diffs(got, outs[i]); if !d.isEmpty { diffs.append("\(t["name"] as! String) step \(i): \(d.prefix(2))") }
            }
        }
        CT.check("all trips, step by step", diffs, [])

        CT.section("chunk plans")
        diffs = []
        for c in f["chunkPlans"] as! [[String: Any]] {
            let got = W.planChunks(size: u64(c["size"]), chunkSize: c["chunkSize"] as! Int).map { [Int($0.offset), $0.length] }
            if got != (c["out"] as! [[Int]]) { diffs.append(c["name"] as! String) }
        }
        CT.check("every plan", diffs, [])

        CT.section("3000 random event scripts: nothing traps, the count never passes the size, an abort lands in idle, only a click starts a transfer")
        var rnd = Lcg(s: 20261014)
        let ids = [bytes(String(repeating: "01", count: 16)), bytes(String(repeating: "02", count: 16))]
        let names = ["a.txt", "../x", "CON", "a\u{0}"], sizes: [UInt64] = [0, 5, 10, 1 << 32, (1 << 32) + 1], offsets: [UInt64] = [0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10]
        var bad: String?
        outer: for _ in 0..<3000 {
            var state = LinkFlow.ReceiverState.idle
            for _ in 0..<(1 + rnd.next(29)) {
                let id = ids[rnd.next(2)]
                let ev: LinkFlow.Event
                switch rnd.next(6) {
                case 0: ev = .frame(.offer(id: id, size: sizes[rnd.next(5)], name: names[rnd.next(4)], kind: ""))
                case 1: ev = .frame(.chunk(id: id, offset: offsets[rnd.next(6)], data: Data(repeating: 0xAB, count: 1 + rnd.next(4))))
                case 2: ev = .frame([.done(id: id), .cancel(id: id), .accept(id: id)][rnd.next(3)])
                case 3: ev = .frame(.decline(id: id, reason: 0))
                case 4: ev = .user([.accept, .decline, .cancel][rnd.next(3)])
                default: ev = .timeout
                }
                let before = state
                let res = LinkFlow.receiverStep(state, ev)
                state = res.state
                var startedByClick = true
                if case .receiving = state, !{ if case .receiving = before { return true }; return false }() { startedByClick = (ev == .user(.accept)) }
                var overshoot = false
                if case .receiving(_, let size, let got) = state { overshoot = got > size }
                if (res.abort != nil && state != .idle) || overshoot || !startedByClick { bad = "\(before) \(ev) -> \(res)"; break outer }
            }
        }
        CT.check("no script breaks an invariant", bad, nil)
        CT.finish()
    }
}
````

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkPairing.swift`**

````swift
import CryptoKit
import Foundation

// Pairing (sub-project G2, docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md "The pairing code"): the commitment, the six-digit code, the clocks, and the two machines that run commit, nonce, reveal and confirm one event
// at a time. A port of `commit_of`, `pairing_code`, `pairing_attempt_allowed`, `pairing_window_open`, `offer_expired`, `pair_responder_step` and `pair_initiator_step` of
// tests/fixtures/link/reference.py, replayed against pairing-cases.json by scripts/test-link-pairing.sh. Foundation and CryptoKit only; the machines draw nothing: the clock ("now",
// whole milliseconds on a monotonic clock) and the random values (a nonce) come in with the event, so the session (LinkSession) is the only place that reads a clock or a random source.
//
// The code is the same on both screens only when both machines saw the same two nonces and the same two static keys. A key is stored only when both users said yes (`notify: .store`).

enum LinkPairing {
    static let codeDigits = 6
    static let commitLabel = Data("notchmeow-link pairing commit v1".utf8)
    static let codeLabel = Data("notchmeow-link pairing code v1".utf8)
    static let gapMs = 30_000             // at most one pairing attempt every 30 seconds
    static let windowMs = 120_000         // the pairing window closes after 2 minutes
    static let offerTimeoutMs = 120_000   // an offer nobody answers is declined after 2 minutes

    /// `SHA-256(label + static + nonce)`, sent in the clear as the payload of the first Noise message. Nil unless both are 32 bytes.
    static func commitOf(nonce: Data, staticKey: Data) -> Data? {
        guard nonce.count == 32, staticKey.count == 32 else { return nil }
        return Data(SHA256.hash(data: commitLabel + staticKey + nonce))
    }

    /// The six digits both screens show: the first 8 bytes of `SHA-256(label + sI + sR + nI + nR)` as a big-endian number, modulo 10^6, with leading zeros. The handshake hash is deliberately
    /// not an input (the contract says why). Nil unless all four are 32 bytes.
    static func code(nonceI: Data, nonceR: Data, staticI: Data, staticR: Data) -> String? {
        guard [nonceI, nonceR, staticI, staticR].allSatisfy({ $0.count == 32 }) else { return nil }
        let d = Array(SHA256.hash(data: codeLabel + staticI + staticR + nonceI + nonceR))
        let n = LinkBytes.readUInt64(d[0..<8]) % 1_000_000
        let s = String(n)
        return String(repeating: "0", count: codeDigits - s.count) + s
    }

    // MARK: The clocks, in whole milliseconds on a monotonic clock

    private static func ms(_ v: Int?) -> Bool { v != nil && v! >= 0 }

    /// Allowed at exactly 30 s after the last attempt; the first attempt (last nil) always; a clock that went back or a value that is not whole milliseconds, never.
    static func attemptAllowed(last: Int?, now: Int?) -> Bool {
        guard ms(now), last == nil || ms(last) else { return false }
        return last == nil || now! - last! >= gapMs
    }

    /// Open below 2 minutes after `start`.
    static func windowOpen(start: Int?, now: Int?) -> Bool { ms(start) && ms(now) && (0..<windowMs).contains(now! - start!) }

    /// Expired at exactly 2 minutes; a clock that went back or a bad value counts as expired: declining is the safe side.
    static func offerExpired(offered: Int?, now: Int?) -> Bool {
        guard ms(offered), ms(now) else { return true }
        return !(0..<offerTimeoutMs).contains(now! - offered!)
    }

    // MARK: Who may start a pairing with this machine

    /// The owner's rule (2026-10-15): an incoming pairing is allowed **only while Settings → Devices is open on this Mac, or within 2 minutes after the user clicked "Pair a machine" on this Mac**
    /// (the contract's window, so it closes at exactly 120 s like `windowOpen`). At any other time the hello is refused silently: nothing is sent, the connection closes, no card, no alert.
    struct Gate: Equatable, Sendable {
        private(set) var devicesOpen = 0
        private(set) var clickedAt: Int?
        mutating func devicesAppeared() { devicesOpen += 1 }
        mutating func devicesDisappeared() { devicesOpen = max(0, devicesOpen - 1) }
        mutating func pairClicked(now: Int) { clickedAt = now }
        func allows(now: Int?) -> Bool { devicesOpen > 0 || LinkPairing.windowOpen(start: clickedAt, now: now) }
    }

    // MARK: The two machines

    enum Refusal: String, Equatable, Sendable {
        case commitLength = "commit-length", commitReplayed = "commit-replayed", attemptTooSoon = "attempt-too-soon", noCommit = "no-commit"
        case revealLate = "reveal-late", revealLength = "reveal-length", revealMismatch = "reveal-mismatch"
        case deviceId = "device-id", ownId = "own-id", idKeyMismatch = "id-key-mismatch", unexpected
        case startLength = "start-length", nonceRepeated = "nonce-repeated", nonceLength = "nonce-length"
        case windowClosed = "window-closed", saidNo = "said-no"
    }

    enum Notify: String, Equatable, Sendable {
        case nonceSent = "nonce-sent", commitSent = "commit-sent", showCode = "show-code", store, discard
    }

    enum Send: Equatable, Sendable {
        case nonce(String), commit(String), reveal(String), pair(yes: Bool)
    }

    /// The two inputs a machine keeps from one step to the next: its own device id and the devices it has paired (device id to the static key stored for it, both lower-case hex).
    /// `paired` is nil when it was not given (an empty map is a different state in the fixtures).
    struct Ids: Equatable, Sendable {
        var ownId: LinkOwnId = .absent
        var paired: [String: String]? = nil
        init(ownId: LinkOwnId = .absent, paired: [String: String]? = nil) { self.ownId = ownId; self.paired = paired }
    }

    struct Out<State: Equatable & Sendable>: Equatable, Sendable {
        var state: State
        var send: [Send] = []
        var notify: Notify? = nil
        var because: Refusal? = nil
    }

    /// False when the machine's own id, if it has one, is not 32 lower-case hex digits (a null one included), or a paired id is not 32 or a paired key is not 64. Fail closed: a capital
    /// letter is never lower-cased, so it cannot make a paired id look new.
    static func idsCanonical(_ ids: Ids) -> Bool {
        switch ids.ownId {
        case .absent: break
        case .null: return false
        case .value(let v): if !LinkBytes.isIdHex(v) { return false }
        }
        return (ids.paired ?? [:]).allSatisfy { LinkBytes.isIdHex($0.key) && LinkBytes.isHex32($0.value) }
    }

    /// Why a peer's hello is refused, given the machine's own id and its paired devices. A hello with no valid device id is refused as `lengthReason` whatever the machine knows; the
    /// all-zero id as `device-id`; a machine whose ids are not canonical refuses every hello as `device-id`; then `own-id`, then `id-key-mismatch` (paired under another key). The key
    /// compared is the one the Noise handshake authenticated, never a value in the hello.
    static func peerRefusal(_ ids: Ids, peerId: String?, peerStatic: String, lengthReason: Refusal) -> Refusal? {
        guard LinkBytes.isIdHex(peerId), let peerId else { return lengthReason }
        if peerId == LinkBytes.zeroId { return .deviceId }
        if !idsCanonical(ids) { return .deviceId }
        if case .value(let own) = ids.ownId, own == peerId { return .ownId }
        if let stored = ids.paired?[peerId], stored != peerStatic { return .idKeyMismatch }
        return nil
    }

    /// A click, or the other side's `pair-yes` or `pair-no`, after the code is shown. Anything but exactly "yes" is a no. Both sides share it.
    private struct Coded { var start: Int; var code: String; var mine: Bool; var theirs: Bool }

    private static func answer<S: Equatable & Sendable>(_ c: Coded, mineAnswer: Bool?, theirAnswer: Bool?, now: Int?, idle: S, coded: (Coded) -> S) -> Out<S> {
        guard windowOpen(start: c.start, now: now) else { return Out(state: idle, notify: .discard, because: .windowClosed) }
        let isMine = mineAnswer != nil
        let yes = isMine ? mineAnswer! : theirAnswer!
        guard yes else { return Out(state: idle, send: isMine ? [.pair(yes: false)] : [], notify: .discard, because: .saidNo) }
        var n = c
        if isMine { n.mine = true } else { n.theirs = true }
        let send: [Send] = isMine ? [.pair(yes: true)] : []
        if n.mine && n.theirs { return Out(state: idle, send: send, notify: .store) }
        return Out(state: coded(n), send: send)
    }

    // MARK: The responder (the machine that is asked to pair)

    struct Seen: Equatable, Sendable { var commit: String; var at: Int }

    enum ResponderPhase: Equatable, Sendable {
        case idle
        case committed(commit: String, start: Int, nonce: String)
        case coded(start: Int, code: String, mine: Bool, theirs: Bool)
    }

    struct ResponderState: Equatable, Sendable {
        var phase: ResponderPhase = .idle
        var seen: [Seen] = []      // the recent commitments, kept across connections
        var last: Int? = nil       // the time of the last attempt
        var ids = Ids()
    }

    struct Reveal: Equatable, Sendable {
        var nonce: String?, staticKey: String?, own: String?, peerId: String?
        init(nonce: String?, staticKey: String?, own: String?, peerId: String?) { self.nonce = nonce; self.staticKey = staticKey; self.own = own; self.peerId = peerId }
    }

    enum ResponderEvent: Equatable, Sendable {
        case commit(commit: String?, nonce: String?, now: Int?)    // the first message arrived; nonce is the app's fresh one
        case reveal(Reveal, now: Int?)                             // the third message was opened: the initiator's nonce, static key and device id; this machine's own static key
        case user(yes: Bool, now: Int?)
        case peer(yes: Bool, now: Int?)
        case unknown
    }

    static func responderStep(_ state: ResponderState, _ event: ResponderEvent) -> Out<ResponderState> {
        var idle = state; idle.phase = .idle
        func refuse(_ r: Refusal) -> Out<ResponderState> { Out(state: idle, because: r) }
        switch event {
        case .commit(let commit, let nonce, let now):
            guard case .idle = state.phase else { return refuse(.unexpected) }
            guard LinkBytes.isHex32(commit), LinkBytes.isHex32(nonce), let commit, let nonce else { return refuse(.commitLength) }
            if state.seen.contains(where: { $0.commit == commit && windowOpen(start: $0.at, now: now) }) { return refuse(.commitReplayed) }   // before the gap, so a replay is named as one
            guard attemptAllowed(last: state.last, now: now) else { return refuse(.attemptTooSoon) }
            var next = state
            next.seen = state.seen.filter { windowOpen(start: $0.at, now: now) } + [Seen(commit: commit, at: now!)]
            next.last = now
            next.phase = .committed(commit: commit, start: now!, nonce: nonce)
            return Out(state: next, send: [.nonce(nonce)], notify: .nonceSent)
        case .reveal(let r, let now):
            switch state.phase {
            case .idle: return refuse(.noCommit)
            case .coded: return refuse(.unexpected)
            case .committed(let commit, let start, let myNonce):
                guard windowOpen(start: start, now: now) else { return refuse(.revealLate) }
                guard LinkBytes.isHex32(r.nonce), LinkBytes.isHex32(r.staticKey), LinkBytes.isHex32(r.own), let rn = r.nonce, let rs = r.staticKey, let own = r.own else { return refuse(.revealLength) }
                guard let want = commitOf(nonce: LinkBytes.data(hex: rn)!, staticKey: LinkBytes.data(hex: rs)!), LinkBytes.hex(want) == commit else { return refuse(.revealMismatch) }   // a wrong nonce and a wrong key look the same, on purpose
                if let why = peerRefusal(state.ids, peerId: r.peerId, peerStatic: rs, lengthReason: .revealLength) { return refuse(why) }
                guard let code = code(nonceI: LinkBytes.data(hex: rn)!, nonceR: LinkBytes.data(hex: myNonce)!, staticI: LinkBytes.data(hex: rs)!, staticR: LinkBytes.data(hex: own)!) else { return refuse(.revealLength) }
                var next = state
                next.phase = .coded(start: start, code: code, mine: false, theirs: false)
                return Out(state: next, notify: .showCode)
            }
        case .user(let yes, let now), .peer(let yes, let now):
            guard case .coded(let start, let code, let mine, let theirs) = state.phase else { return Out(state: state) }   // an answer before there is a code is ignored
            var isUser = false
            if case .user = event { isUser = true }
            let c = Coded(start: start, code: code, mine: mine, theirs: theirs)
            return answer(c, mineAnswer: isUser ? yes : nil, theirAnswer: isUser ? nil : yes, now: now, idle: idle) { n in
                var s = state; s.phase = .coded(start: n.start, code: n.code, mine: n.mine, theirs: n.theirs); return s
            }
        case .unknown:
            return Out(state: state)
        }
    }

    // MARK: The initiator (the machine whose user started the pairing)

    enum InitiatorPhase: Equatable, Sendable {
        case idle
        case started(nonce: String, staticKey: String, start: Int)
        case coded(start: Int, code: String, mine: Bool, theirs: Bool)
    }

    struct InitiatorState: Equatable, Sendable {
        var phase: InitiatorPhase = .idle
        var last: Int? = nil
        var ids = Ids()
    }

    enum InitiatorEvent: Equatable, Sendable {
        case start(nonce: String?, staticKey: String?, now: Int?)                                   // the user chose Pair a machine; the app's fresh nonce and its static public key
        case nonce(String?, peerStatic: String?, peerId: String?, now: Int?)                        // the second message was opened: the responder's nonce, static key and device id
        case user(yes: Bool, now: Int?)
        case peer(yes: Bool, now: Int?)
        case unknown
    }

    static func initiatorStep(_ state: InitiatorState, _ event: InitiatorEvent) -> Out<InitiatorState> {
        var idle = state; idle.phase = .idle
        func refuse(_ r: Refusal) -> Out<InitiatorState> { Out(state: idle, because: r) }
        switch event {
        case .start(let nonce, let staticKey, let now):
            guard case .idle = state.phase else { return refuse(.unexpected) }
            guard LinkBytes.isHex32(nonce), LinkBytes.isHex32(staticKey), let nonce, let staticKey else { return refuse(.startLength) }
            guard attemptAllowed(last: state.last, now: now) else { return refuse(.attemptTooSoon) }
            var next = state
            next.last = now
            next.phase = .started(nonce: nonce, staticKey: staticKey, start: now!)
            let c = commitOf(nonce: LinkBytes.data(hex: nonce)!, staticKey: LinkBytes.data(hex: staticKey)!)!
            return Out(state: next, send: [.commit(LinkBytes.hex(c))], notify: .commitSent)
        case .nonce(let nonce, let peerStatic, let peerId, let now):
            switch state.phase {
            case .idle: return refuse(.noCommit)
            case .coded: return refuse(.nonceRepeated)
            case .started(let myNonce, let myStatic, let start):
                guard windowOpen(start: start, now: now) else { return refuse(.revealLate) }
                guard LinkBytes.isHex32(nonce), LinkBytes.isHex32(peerStatic), let nonce, let peerStatic else { return refuse(.nonceLength) }
                if let why = peerRefusal(state.ids, peerId: peerId, peerStatic: peerStatic, lengthReason: .nonceLength) { return refuse(why) }
                guard let code = code(nonceI: LinkBytes.data(hex: myNonce)!, nonceR: LinkBytes.data(hex: nonce)!, staticI: LinkBytes.data(hex: myStatic)!, staticR: LinkBytes.data(hex: peerStatic)!) else { return refuse(.nonceLength) }
                var next = state
                next.phase = .coded(start: start, code: code, mine: false, theirs: false)
                return Out(state: next, send: [.reveal(myNonce)], notify: .showCode)
            }
        case .user(let yes, let now), .peer(let yes, let now):
            guard case .coded(let start, let code, let mine, let theirs) = state.phase else { return Out(state: state) }
            var isUser = false
            if case .user = event { isUser = true }
            let c = Coded(start: start, code: code, mine: mine, theirs: theirs)
            return answer(c, mineAnswer: isUser ? yes : nil, theirAnswer: isUser ? nil : yes, now: now, idle: idle) { n in
                var s = state; s.phase = .coded(start: n.start, code: n.code, mine: n.mine, theirs: n.theirs); return s
            }
        case .unknown:
            return Out(state: state)
        }
    }
}
````

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkFlow.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// What each side of a transfer does with each frame (sub-project G2, docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md "What each side does with each frame"): `receiverStep` and `senderStep` are pure. A state, one event
// (a frame, a click, a timeout), and the answer: the next state, the frames to send, what to tell the user, and why the connection must close. A port of `receiver_step` and `sender_step` of
// tests/fixtures/link/reference.py, replayed against flow-cases.json by scripts/test-link-flow.sh. Foundation only (the sanitizer is LinkName).
//
// Only a click (`accept`) starts a transfer. One transfer at a time on a connection; the app declines an offer as busy (reason 2) itself when another transfer from the same peer is
// already running on another connection, since the reference models one connection only.

enum LinkFlow {
    typealias Frame = LinkWire.Frame

    enum Notify: String, Equatable, Sendable {
        case offer, expired, withdrawn, cancelled, complete, declined
    }

    /// Why the connection must close. After an abort the state is idle and nothing is saved as complete.
    enum Abort: String, Equatable, Sendable {
        case unexpected, id, order, overflow, short
    }

    enum Decline {
        static let user: UInt8 = 0, timeout: UInt8 = 1, busy: UInt8 = 2, tooLarge: UInt8 = 3, badOffer: UInt8 = 4
    }

    struct Out<State: Equatable & Sendable>: Equatable, Sendable {
        var state: State
        var send: [Frame] = []
        var notify: Notify? = nil
        var abort: Abort? = nil
    }

    enum UserAction: Equatable, Sendable { case accept, decline, cancel }

    // MARK: The receiver

    /// `name` is the sanitized name, the one that is shown and saved.
    enum ReceiverState: Equatable, Sendable {
        case idle
        case offered(id: Data, size: UInt64, name: String)
        case receiving(id: Data, size: UInt64, got: UInt64)
    }

    enum Event: Equatable, Sendable {
        case frame(Frame)
        case user(UserAction)
        case timeout
    }

    static func receiverStep(_ state: ReceiverState, _ event: Event) -> Out<ReceiverState> {
        switch event {
        case .user(let u):
            switch (state, u) {
            case (.offered(let id, let size, _), .accept): return Out(state: .receiving(id: id, size: size, got: 0), send: [.accept(id: id)])
            case (.offered(let id, _, _), .decline): return Out(state: .idle, send: [.decline(id: id, reason: Decline.user)])
            case (.receiving(let id, _, _), .cancel), (.receiving(let id, _, _), .decline): return Out(state: .idle, send: [.cancel(id: id)], notify: .cancelled)
            default: return Out(state: state)
            }
        case .timeout:
            if case .offered(let id, _, _) = state { return Out(state: .idle, send: [.decline(id: id, reason: Decline.timeout)], notify: .expired) }
            return Out(state: state)
        case .frame(let f):
            switch f {
            case .pairYes, .pairNo:
                return Out(state: .idle, abort: .unexpected)            // a pairing answer has no place in a transfer
            case .offer(let id, let size, let rawName, _):
                guard case .idle = state else { return Out(state: state, send: [.decline(id: id, reason: Decline.busy)]) }
                if size > LinkWire.sizeMax { return Out(state: .idle, send: [.decline(id: id, reason: Decline.tooLarge)]) }
                guard case .ok(let name) = LinkName.sanitize(rawName) else { return Out(state: .idle, send: [.decline(id: id, reason: Decline.badOffer)]) }
                return Out(state: .offered(id: id, size: size, name: name), notify: .offer)
            case .cancel(let id):
                switch state {
                case .offered(let own, _, _) where own == id: return Out(state: .idle, notify: .withdrawn)
                case .receiving(let own, _, _) where own == id: return Out(state: .idle, notify: .cancelled)
                default: return Out(state: state)                       // a cancel for a transfer we do not know is harmless
                }
            case .chunk(let id, let offset, let data):
                guard case .receiving(let own, let size, let got) = state else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .unexpected) }
                guard id == own else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .id) }
                guard offset == got else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .order) }          // a replayed chunk or a skipped one
                guard got + UInt64(data.count) <= size else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .overflow) }
                return Out(state: .receiving(id: own, size: size, got: got + UInt64(data.count)))
            case .done(let id):
                guard case .receiving(let own, let size, let got) = state else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .unexpected) }
                guard id == own else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .id) }
                guard got == size else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .short) }
                return Out(state: .idle, notify: .complete)
            case .accept, .decline:
                return Out(state: .idle, abort: .unexpected)
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: The sender

    enum SenderState: Equatable, Sendable {
        case idle
        case offered(id: Data)
        case sending(id: Data)
    }

    enum SenderEvent: Equatable, Sendable {
        case frame(Frame)
        case userCancel
        case userOther
    }

    static func senderStep(_ state: SenderState, _ event: SenderEvent) -> Out<SenderState> {
        let current: Data?
        switch state { case .idle: current = nil; case .offered(let id), .sending(let id): current = id }
        switch event {
        case .userCancel:
            if let id = current { return Out(state: .idle, send: [.cancel(id: id)], notify: .cancelled) }
            return Out(state: state)
        case .userOther:
            return Out(state: state)
        case .frame(let f):
            var fid: Data?
            switch f {
            case .accept(let id), .done(let id), .cancel(let id), .decline(let id, _), .chunk(let id, _, _), .offer(let id, _, _, _): fid = id
            case .pairYes, .pairNo: fid = nil
            }
            if case .cancel = f, current == nil { return Out(state: state) }   // a cancel with no transfer running is ignored
            switch f {
            case .offer, .chunk, .done, .pairYes, .pairNo: return Out(state: .idle, abort: .unexpected)
            default: break
            }
            guard let current else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .unexpected) }
            guard fid == current else { return Out(state: .idle, abort: .id) }
            switch (state, f) {
            case (.offered, .accept): return Out(state: .sending(id: current))
            case (.offered, .decline): return Out(state: .idle, notify: .declined)
            case (_, .cancel): return Out(state: .idle, notify: .cancelled)
            default: return Out(state: .idle, abort: .unexpected)
            }
        }
    }
}
````
