### Task 4: `LinkKeychain`, the settings and the device list

**Agent:** `nm-implementer` (Haiku 5.5, high). The code is given in full; the tests prove it.

**Files:**
- Create: `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkKeychain.swift`, `LinkSettings.swift`; `tests/LinkKeychainTests.swift`, `tests/LinkSettingsTests.swift`; `scripts/test-link-keychain.sh`, `scripts/test-link-settings.sh`
- Modify: `NotchBuddy/NotchBuddy.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` (regenerated)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LinkBytes`, `LinkName`, `LinkWire` (Task 2).
- Produces: `LinkSecretStore` (`get`, `set`, `remove`, `accounts(prefix:)`), `SystemSecretStore(service:)` (`appService`), `MemorySecretStore`, `LinkPeer {id, name, key, at}`, `LinkKeychainError`, `LinkKeychain` (`staticKey()`, `staticPublicHex()`, `deviceId()`, `peers()`, `pairedKeys()`, `peer(_:)`, `add(_:ownId:)`, `forget(_:)`, the three account names), `LinkSettingsBacking`, `LinkSettings` (`visible`, `deviceName`, `Key`, `clean`, `load`, `save`, `defaultName`, `validName`), `LinkDirectory.rows(adverts:peers:ownId:) -> [Row]`.

**What the Keychain test touches.** `test-link-keychain.sh` runs everything on a memory store, then once on the real Keychain under a service name made for the run (`io.github.aikusan.notchmeow.test.link.<uuid>`), and deletes every item it made. It never touches the app's own service, the Debug build's, or the normal one. `NM_SKIP_REAL_KEYCHAIN=1` skips the real part (a runner with no unlocked keychain). Run it in the background with its output in a file: an unsigned program can make macOS ask for the keychain's password, and a prompt would otherwise hang the step; if a prompt appears, stop and report.

**Decisions here (the owner's answers, below):** the device id lives in the Keychain beside the key (`link.device-id`) so that both survive together and are lost together; the two settings are in UserDefaults; **a Debug build uses the service name `io.github.aikusan.notchmeow.debug`** (`#if DEBUG && !APPSTORE` in `SystemSecretStore`), so a Debug build, its fake peer and the tests can never write under the real name, and the Keychain test checks that its own service is neither; a peer item that does not read back clean is skipped, never trusted and never deleted; a key item of the wrong size is `corrupt` and is **never replaced** (the old key may still be wanted).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests and their scripts**

**Create `tests/LinkKeychainTests.swift`**

````swift
import CryptoKit
import Foundation

// LinkKeychain: the three kinds of item, the checks at store time, and a round trip through the real Keychain under a service name that exists only for this test (and is deleted again).
// Run it through scripts/test-link-keychain.sh. NM_SKIP_REAL_KEYCHAIN=1 skips the part that touches the Keychain.

@main
enum LinkKeychainTests {
    static let peerKey = String(repeating: "c1", count: 32)
    static let peerId = String(repeating: "e1", count: 16)
    static let ownId = String(repeating: "d1", count: 16)
    static func peer(_ id: String = peerId, key: String = peerKey, name: String = "Studio PC") -> LinkPeer { LinkPeer(id: id, name: name, key: key, at: 1_760_000_000) }

    static func thrown(_ f: () throws -> Void) -> LinkKeychainError? {
        do { try f(); return nil } catch { return error as? LinkKeychainError }
    }

    @MainActor static func suite(_ label: String, _ store: any LinkSecretStore) {
        let kc = LinkKeychain(store: store)
        CT.section("\(label): this machine's key and id")
        let k1 = try! kc.staticKey(), k2 = try! kc.staticKey()
        CT.check("the static key is made once and read back, 32 bytes", [k1.rawRepresentation == k2.rawRepresentation, k1.rawRepresentation.count == 32], [true, true])
        CT.check("its public half is 64 lower-case hex digits", LinkBytes.isHex32(try! kc.staticPublicHex()), true)
        let id = try! kc.deviceId()
        CT.check("the device id is 32 lower-case hex digits, not zero, and stays", [LinkBytes.isIdHex(id), id != LinkBytes.zeroId, (try! kc.deviceId()) == id], [true, true, true])

        CT.section("\(label): paired machines")
        CT.check("nothing paired at first", kc.peers().count, 0)
        try! kc.add(peer(), ownId: ownId)
        CT.check("a stored machine reads back as it was", kc.peers(), [peerId: peer()])
        CT.check("the paired map is id to key", kc.pairedKeys(), [peerId: peerKey])
        try! kc.add(peer(name: "Studio PC 2"), ownId: ownId)
        CT.check("the same id under the same key refreshes the name", kc.peer(peerId)?.name, "Studio PC 2")
        CT.check("the same id under another key is refused, and the stored key stays", [thrown { try kc.add(peer(key: String(repeating: "f1", count: 32)), ownId: ownId) }, kc.peer(peerId)?.key == peerKey] as [AnyHashable?], [LinkKeychainError.idKeyMismatch, true] as [AnyHashable?])
        CT.check("this machine's own id, the zero id, a capital id and a short key are refused", [
            thrown { try kc.add(peer(ownId), ownId: ownId) }, thrown { try kc.add(peer(LinkBytes.zeroId), ownId: ownId) },
            thrown { try kc.add(peer(String(repeating: "E1", count: 16)), ownId: ownId) }, thrown { try kc.add(peer(key: "c1c1"), ownId: ownId) },
            thrown { try kc.add(peer(name: " padded"), ownId: ownId) },
        ], [.invalidPeer, .invalidPeer, .invalidPeer, .invalidPeer, .invalidPeer])
        CT.check("a second machine is a second item", { try! kc.add(peer(String(repeating: "e2", count: 16), key: String(repeating: "c2", count: 32), name: "Laptop"), ownId: ownId); return kc.peers().count }(), 2)
        // An item that does not read back clean is skipped, never trusted and never deleted.
        _ = store.set(LinkKeychain.peerPrefix + String(repeating: "E3", count: 16), Data("{}".utf8))
        _ = store.set(LinkKeychain.peerPrefix + String(repeating: "e4", count: 16), Data("not json".utf8))
        _ = store.set(LinkKeychain.peerPrefix + String(repeating: "e5", count: 16), Data("{\"name\":\"x\",\"key\":\"c1\",\"at\":1}".utf8))
        CT.check("a bad item is skipped and left in place", [kc.peers().count, store.accounts(prefix: LinkKeychain.peerPrefix).count], [2, 5])
        kc.forget(peerId)
        CT.check("Forget removes one machine and nothing else", [kc.peer(peerId) == nil ? 0 : 1, kc.peers().count], [0, 1])
        kc.forget("not an id")
        CT.check("Forget with something that is not an id does nothing", kc.peers().count, 1)

        CT.section("\(label): damaged secrets are never replaced")
        _ = store.set(LinkKeychain.staticKeyAccount, Data(count: 5))
        CT.check("a key item of the wrong size is corrupt", thrown { _ = try kc.staticKey() }, .corrupt)
        CT.check("and is still there", store.get(LinkKeychain.staticKeyAccount)?.count, 5)
        _ = store.set(LinkKeychain.deviceIdAccount, Data(count: 16))
        CT.check("an all-zero device id is corrupt", thrown { _ = try kc.deviceId() }, .corrupt)
    }

    @MainActor static func main() {
        suite("memory", MemorySecretStore())

        let memory = MemorySecretStore(); memory.failWrites = true
        CT.section("a write that fails")
        CT.check("is reported, never swallowed", [thrown { _ = try LinkKeychain(store: memory).staticKey() }, thrown { _ = try LinkKeychain(store: memory).deviceId() }, thrown { try LinkKeychain(store: memory).add(peer(), ownId: ownId) }], [.writeFailed, .writeFailed, .writeFailed])

        if ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["NM_SKIP_REAL_KEYCHAIN"] == "1" {
            print("real Keychain: skipped (NM_SKIP_REAL_KEYCHAIN=1)")
        } else {
            let service = "io.github.aikusan.notchmeow.test.link.\(UUID().uuidString)"   // exists only for this run
            let real = SystemSecretStore(service: service)
            CT.checkTrue("the test service name is neither the app's, nor the Debug build's, nor the normal one", service != SystemSecretStore.appService && service != "io.github.aikusan.notchmeow" && service != "io.github.aikusan.notchmeow.debug" && service.contains(".test."))
            suite("real Keychain", real)
            // Delete everything this run made, whatever the checks said.
            for a in real.accounts(prefix: "") { real.remove(a) }
            CT.check("the real Keychain holds nothing of the test any more", real.accounts(prefix: ""), [])
        }
        CT.finish()
    }
}
````

**Create `tests/LinkSettingsTests.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// LinkSettings (what is saved and how it is cleaned) and LinkDirectory (what Devices and the Send menu list). No UserDefaults, no Keychain: a dictionary stands in for the store.
// Run it through scripts/test-link-settings.sh.

final class DictBacking: LinkSettingsBacking {
    var values: [String: Any] = [:]
    func object(forKey key: String) -> Any? { values[key] }
    func set(_ value: Any?, forKey key: String) { values[key] = value }
}

@main
enum LinkSettingsTests {
    @MainActor static func main() {
        CT.section("a Mac that has stored nothing")
        let fresh = LinkSettings.load(from: DictBacking(), host: "Sam's MacBook Pro")
        CT.check("is not visible on the network, and offers its own name", [fresh.visible ? 1 : 0, fresh.deviceName == "Sam's MacBook Pro" ? 1 : 0], [0, 1])

        CT.section("what is stored is cleaned")
        func load(_ visible: Any?, _ name: Any?) -> LinkSettings { LinkSettings.clean(visible: visible, deviceName: name, host: "Studio") }
        CT.check("only the boolean true is visible: 1, \"true\", 1.0, null and a list are not", [load(true, nil).visible, load(1, nil).visible, load("true", nil).visible, load(NSNull(), nil).visible, load([true], nil).visible, load(false, nil).visible], [true, false, false, false, false, false])
        CT.check("a good name is kept", load(nil, "Desk PC").deviceName, "Desk PC")
        CT.check("a name the card would refuse falls back to the default: empty, padded, 64 bytes, a zero-width space, a newline, a number", [load(nil, "").deviceName, load(nil, " x").deviceName, load(nil, String(repeating: "n", count: 64)).deviceName, load(nil, "a\u{200B}b").deviceName, load(nil, "a\nb").deviceName, load(nil, 5).deviceName], Array(repeating: "Studio", count: 6))

        CT.section("the default name")
        CT.check("controls and invisible characters are dropped, the ends trimmed", LinkSettings.defaultName(host: "  Sam\u{200B}'s\tMac \n"), "Sam'sMac")
        CT.check("a long name is cut at a character, to 63 bytes", [LinkSettings.defaultName(host: String(repeating: "\u{1F408}", count: 30)).utf8.count, LinkSettings.defaultName(host: String(repeating: "a", count: 100)).utf8.count], [60, 63])
        CT.check("nothing left is Mac", [LinkSettings.defaultName(host: ""), LinkSettings.defaultName(host: " \u{200B}\n")], ["Mac", "Mac"])

        CT.section("save and load")
        let store = DictBacking()
        LinkSettings(visible: true, deviceName: "Desk PC").save(to: store)
        CT.check("what is saved is read back", LinkSettings.load(from: store, host: "x"), LinkSettings(visible: true, deviceName: "Desk PC"))
        CT.check("under the frozen keys", [store.values["link.visible"] as? Bool, store.values["link.deviceName"] as? String] as [AnyHashable?], [true, "Desk PC"] as [AnyHashable?])

        CT.section("the directory")
        let own = String(repeating: "d1", count: 16)
        func id(_ n: UInt8) -> String { String(repeating: String(format: "%02x", n), count: 16) }
        func peer(_ n: UInt8, _ name: String) -> LinkPeer { LinkPeer(id: id(n), name: name, key: String(repeating: "c1", count: 32), at: 1) }
        let adverts = [LinkWire.Advert(id: id(1), name: "zed's PC"), LinkWire.Advert(id: id(2), name: "Laptop (advert name)"), LinkWire.Advert(id: id(3), name: "Bar"), LinkWire.Advert(id: own, name: "This Mac"), LinkWire.Advert(id: id(3), name: "Bar again")]
        let peers = [id(1): peer(1, "Zed PC"), id(2): peer(2, "Alpha Laptop"), id(9): peer(9, "Old Phone")]
        let rows = LinkDirectory.rows(adverts: adverts, peers: peers, ownId: own)
        CT.check("paired online by name, then paired offline, then only seen; the advert's name never replaces a paired name; this Mac is not listed; a repeated advert counts once",
                 rows.map { "\($0.name)|\($0.state)" }, ["Alpha Laptop|pairedOnline", "Zed PC|pairedOnline", "Old Phone|pairedOffline", "Bar|available"])
        CT.check("nothing seen, nothing paired: no rows", LinkDirectory.rows(adverts: [], peers: [:], ownId: own), [])
        CT.finish()
    }
}
````

**Create `scripts/test-link-keychain.sh`**

````bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# LinkKeychain (the static key, the device id, one item per paired machine): on a memory store, and on the real Keychain under a service name made for the run and deleted at the end.
# NM_SKIP_REAL_KEYCHAIN=1 skips the real Keychain (a runner with no unlocked keychain).
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
TEST_DIR="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/notchmeow-link-keychain.XXXXXX")"
trap 'rm -rf "$TEST_DIR"' EXIT
export NM_LOG_DIR="$TEST_DIR/logs"   # tests never write to ~/Library/Logs

APP=NotchBuddy/Sources/App
swiftc -swift-version 6 \
    $APP/LinkBytes.swift $APP/LinkName.swift $APP/LinkFrames.swift $APP/LinkKeychain.swift \
    tests/CatTestSupport.swift tests/LinkKeychainTests.swift \
    -o "$TEST_DIR/link-keychain-tests"
"$TEST_DIR/link-keychain-tests"
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the implementation**

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkKeychain.swift`**

````swift
import CryptoKit
import Foundation
import Security

// The link's secrets (sub-project G2): this machine's static private key, its device id, and one item for each machine it has paired. They live in the Keychain and nowhere else: never in a
// file, a default, a log or a fixture (docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md "Notes for ports"). The three account names are frozen like the pill IDs:
//   `link.static-key`   32 bytes, the Curve25519 private key of the Noise handshakes
//   `link.device-id`    16 bytes, this machine's random device id (not a secret, but it must survive with the key)
//   `link.peer.<id>`    one JSON object {name, key, at} for each paired machine, <id> being its 32 lower-case hex digits
// `LinkKeychain` takes the store it reads and writes, so the tests run it on a memory store and, under a test-only service name, on the real Keychain.

protocol LinkSecretStore: Sendable {
    func get(_ account: String) -> Data?
    /// False when the item could not be written.
    func set(_ account: String, _ data: Data) -> Bool
    func remove(_ account: String)
    /// Every account whose name starts with `prefix`.
    func accounts(prefix: String) -> [String]
}

/// The system Keychain, as a generic password under `service`. The strictest access the app already uses for its keys (`Keychain` in ClaudeService.swift): only while the Mac is unlocked, never synced
/// to iCloud, never migrated to another device.
struct SystemSecretStore: LinkSecretStore {
    /// The app's Keychain service name: the value of `Keychain.service` in ClaudeService.swift (scripts/test-link-wiring.sh pins that the two are the same text). **A Debug build keeps everything of
    /// the link under a service name of its own** (the owner's rule, 2026-10-15), chosen at compile time, so a Debug build, its fake peer and its tests can never write under the real name.
    /// Release and App Store builds (and the test programs, which are not built with DEBUG) use the normal one. `scripts/check-app-binary.sh` proves the debug string is absent from them.
    #if DEBUG && !APPSTORE
    static let appService = "io.github.aikusan.notchmeow.debug"
    #else
    static let appService = "io.github.aikusan.notchmeow"
    #endif
    let service: String
    init(service: String = SystemSecretStore.appService) { self.service = service }

    private func base(_ account: String?) -> [String: Any] {
        var q: [String: Any] = [kSecClass as String: kSecClassGenericPassword, kSecAttrService as String: service]
        if let account { q[kSecAttrAccount as String] = account }
        return q
    }

    func get(_ account: String) -> Data? {
        var q = base(account)
        q[kSecReturnData as String] = true
        q[kSecMatchLimit as String] = kSecMatchLimitOne
        var out: AnyObject?
        guard SecItemCopyMatching(q as CFDictionary, &out) == errSecSuccess else { return nil }
        return out as? Data
    }

    func set(_ account: String, _ data: Data) -> Bool {
        SecItemDelete(base(account) as CFDictionary)
        var item = base(account)
        item[kSecValueData as String] = data
        item[kSecAttrAccessible as String] = kSecAttrAccessibleWhenUnlockedThisDeviceOnly
        item[kSecAttrSynchronizable as String] = kCFBooleanFalse!
        return SecItemAdd(item as CFDictionary, nil) == errSecSuccess
    }

    func remove(_ account: String) { SecItemDelete(base(account) as CFDictionary) }

    func accounts(prefix: String) -> [String] {
        var q = base(nil)
        q[kSecReturnAttributes as String] = true
        q[kSecMatchLimit as String] = kSecMatchLimitAll
        var out: AnyObject?
        guard SecItemCopyMatching(q as CFDictionary, &out) == errSecSuccess, let rows = out as? [[String: Any]] else { return [] }
        return rows.compactMap { $0[kSecAttrAccount as String] as? String }.filter { $0.hasPrefix(prefix) }
    }
}

/// A store in memory, for the tests and for the Debug fake peer: nothing here touches the Keychain.
final class MemorySecretStore: LinkSecretStore, @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var items: [String: Data] = [:]
    var failWrites = false
    func get(_ account: String) -> Data? { lock.withLock { items[account] } }
    func set(_ account: String, _ data: Data) -> Bool { lock.withLock { if failWrites { return false }; items[account] = data; return true } }
    func remove(_ account: String) { lock.withLock { _ = items.removeValue(forKey: account) } }
    func accounts(prefix: String) -> [String] { lock.withLock { items.keys.filter { $0.hasPrefix(prefix) }.sorted() } }
}

/// A machine this one has paired.
struct LinkPeer: Equatable, Sendable {
    let id: String      // 32 lower-case hex digits, not all zero
    let name: String    // the authenticated name from its hello
    let key: String     // its static public key, 64 lower-case hex digits
    let at: Int         // when it was paired (seconds since 1970; shown in Devices, never decides anything)
}

enum LinkKeychainError: Error, Equatable {
    case corrupt          // an item exists and is not what it should be: never replaced silently (the old key may still be wanted)
    case writeFailed
    case invalidPeer      // not canonical, the zero id, or this machine's own id
    case idKeyMismatch    // the id is already paired under another key: forget it first
}

struct LinkKeychain: Sendable {
    static let staticKeyAccount = "link.static-key"
    static let deviceIdAccount = "link.device-id"
    static let peerPrefix = "link.peer."

    let store: any LinkSecretStore
    init(store: any LinkSecretStore = SystemSecretStore()) { self.store = store }

    // MARK: This machine

    /// The static private key; made on first use (32 random bytes from CryptoKit) and written at once.
    func staticKey() throws -> Curve25519.KeyAgreement.PrivateKey {
        if let raw = store.get(Self.staticKeyAccount) {
            guard raw.count == 32, let k = try? Curve25519.KeyAgreement.PrivateKey(rawRepresentation: raw) else { throw LinkKeychainError.corrupt }
            return k
        }
        let k = Curve25519.KeyAgreement.PrivateKey()
        guard store.set(Self.staticKeyAccount, k.rawRepresentation) else { throw LinkKeychainError.writeFailed }
        return k
    }

    func staticPublicHex() throws -> String { LinkBytes.hex(try staticKey().publicKey.rawRepresentation) }

    /// This machine's device id (32 lower-case hex digits, never all zero); made on first use.
    func deviceId() throws -> String {
        if let raw = store.get(Self.deviceIdAccount) {
            let id = LinkBytes.hex(raw)
            guard raw.count == LinkWire.idLen, id != LinkBytes.zeroId else { throw LinkKeychainError.corrupt }
            return id
        }
        var id: Data
        repeat { id = Data((0..<LinkWire.idLen).map { _ in UInt8.random(in: 0...255) }) } while id.allSatisfy { $0 == 0 }
        guard store.set(Self.deviceIdAccount, id) else { throw LinkKeychainError.writeFailed }
        return LinkBytes.hex(id)
    }

    // MARK: Paired machines

    private struct Stored: Codable { let name: String; let key: String; let at: Int }

    /// Every paired machine that reads back clean. An item that does not (a wrong account name, a key that is not 64 lower-case hex digits, a name the card would refuse) is skipped, not
    /// deleted, and not trusted.
    func peers() -> [String: LinkPeer] {
        var out: [String: LinkPeer] = [:]
        for account in store.accounts(prefix: Self.peerPrefix) {
            let id = String(account.dropFirst(Self.peerPrefix.count))
            guard LinkBytes.isIdHex(id), id != LinkBytes.zeroId, let raw = store.get(account), let s = try? JSONDecoder().decode(Stored.self, from: raw),
                  LinkBytes.isHex32(s.key), (try? LinkWire.deviceName(Array(s.name.utf8))) != nil else { continue }
            out[id] = LinkPeer(id: id, name: s.name, key: s.key, at: s.at)
        }
        return out
    }

    /// The paired map the pairing machines take: device id to static key, lower-case hex.
    func pairedKeys() -> [String: String] { peers().mapValues(\.key) }

    func peer(_ id: String) -> LinkPeer? { peers()[id] }

    /// Stores a machine after a pairing both users confirmed. The paired map is read **again here**, not taken from the snapshot the pairing started with: two pairings can overlap for up to two
    /// minutes (docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md). Re-pairing the same id under the same key refreshes the name.
    func add(_ p: LinkPeer, ownId: String) throws {
        guard LinkBytes.isIdHex(p.id), p.id != LinkBytes.zeroId, p.id != ownId, LinkBytes.isHex32(p.key), (try? LinkWire.deviceName(Array(p.name.utf8))) != nil else { throw LinkKeychainError.invalidPeer }
        if let existing = peer(p.id), existing.key != p.key { throw LinkKeychainError.idKeyMismatch }
        let data = try JSONEncoder().encode(Stored(name: p.name, key: p.key, at: p.at))
        guard store.set(Self.peerPrefix + p.id, data) else { throw LinkKeychainError.writeFailed }
    }

    func forget(_ id: String) {
        guard LinkBytes.isIdHex(id) else { return }
        store.remove(Self.peerPrefix + id)
    }
}
````

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkSettings.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// What the link saves and how it is cleaned (sub-project G2): whether this Mac is visible on the network, and the name the other machines show for it. Two keys in UserDefaults:
// `link.visible` (a Bool, **off until the user turns it on** in Settings → Devices: until then there is no listener, no advert and no local-network prompt) and `link.deviceName` (text).
// The keys are frozen like the cat settings' keys. Secrets are not here: the keys and the paired machines are in the Keychain (LinkKeychain). Foundation only.

protocol LinkSettingsBacking {
    func object(forKey key: String) -> Any?
    func set(_ value: Any?, forKey key: String)
}

extension UserDefaults: LinkSettingsBacking {}

struct LinkSettings: Equatable, Sendable {
    var visible = false
    var deviceName: String

    enum Key {
        static let visible = "link.visible", deviceName = "link.deviceName"
    }

    /// `true` only for the boolean true: the number 1 and the text "true" are off (a preferences file can be hand-edited).
    static func isTrue(_ v: Any?) -> Bool {
        guard let n = v as? NSNumber, CFGetTypeID(n) == CFBooleanGetTypeID() else { return false }
        return n.boolValue
    }

    /// A name that the other machine's card would accept (`LinkWire.deviceName`: 1 to 63 bytes, no control, format or invisible character, no space at either end), or nil.
    static func validName(_ v: Any?) -> String? {
        guard let s = v as? String, (try? LinkWire.deviceName(Array(s.utf8))) != nil else { return nil }
        return s
    }

    /// The name offered by default, from the Mac's own name ("Sam's MacBook Pro"): the characters a card would refuse are dropped, the ends trimmed, the rest cut to 63 bytes at a
    /// character. A name with nothing left is "Mac".
    static func defaultName(host: String) -> String {
        var kept: [Unicode.Scalar] = []
        for s in host.unicodeScalars where !LinkName.isBadCategory(s) && !LinkName.isInvisible(s) { kept.append(s) }
        while let f = kept.first, LinkName.isSpace(f) { kept.removeFirst() }
        while let l = kept.last, LinkName.isSpace(l) { kept.removeLast() }
        var out: [Unicode.Scalar] = [], bytes = 0
        for s in kept { if bytes + UTF8.width(s) > LinkWire.deviceNameMax { break }; out.append(s); bytes += UTF8.width(s) }
        while let l = out.last, LinkName.isSpace(l) { out.removeLast() }
        return out.isEmpty ? "Mac" : LinkName.string(out)
    }

    /// Everything stored, cleaned: visible only when exactly `true`, the name when it passes `validName`, else the default from `host`. Never throws.
    static func clean(visible: Any?, deviceName: Any?, host: String) -> LinkSettings {
        LinkSettings(visible: isTrue(visible), deviceName: validName(deviceName) ?? defaultName(host: host))
    }

    static func load(from store: any LinkSettingsBacking, host: String) -> LinkSettings {
        clean(visible: store.object(forKey: Key.visible), deviceName: store.object(forKey: Key.deviceName), host: host)
    }

    func save(to store: any LinkSettingsBacking) {
        store.set(visible, forKey: Key.visible)
        store.set(deviceName, forKey: Key.deviceName)
    }
}

/// What Settings → Devices lists and what the Send menu offers: the machines seen on the network and the machines paired, merged. Pure, so the order and the states are tested.
enum LinkDirectory {
    struct Row: Equatable, Sendable {
        enum State: Equatable, Sendable {
            case pairedOnline       // paired, and its advert is on the network now
            case pairedOffline      // paired, not seen
            case available          // seen, not paired
        }
        let id: String
        let name: String
        let state: State
    }

    /// Paired machines that are online first (a paired machine's name is the authenticated one it gave when it paired, not the advert's), then the paired that are not, then the machines only
    /// seen, each group by name. An advert with this machine's own id is never listed (`LinkWire.parseTxt` refuses it already; this is the second line).
    static func rows(adverts: [LinkWire.Advert], peers: [String: LinkPeer], ownId: String) -> [Row] {
        let seen = Dictionary(adverts.filter { $0.id != ownId }.map { ($0.id, $0) }, uniquingKeysWith: { first, _ in first })
        func byName(_ a: Row, _ b: Row) -> Bool { a.name.localizedStandardCompare(b.name) == .orderedAscending }
        let paired = peers.values.map { Row(id: $0.id, name: $0.name, state: seen[$0.id] != nil ? .pairedOnline : .pairedOffline) }
        let online = paired.filter { $0.state == .pairedOnline }.sorted(by: byName), offline = paired.filter { $0.state == .pairedOffline }.sorted(by: byName)
        let others = seen.values.filter { peers[$0.id] == nil }.map { Row(id: $0.id, name: $0.name, state: .available) }.sorted(by: byName)
        return online + offline + others
    }
}
````

### Task 5: The files: the sink and the source

**Agent:** `nm-implementer` (Haiku 5.5, high). The code is given in full; the tests prove it.

**Files:**
- Create: `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkFiles.swift`, `tests/LinkFilesTests.swift`, `scripts/test-link-files.sh`
- Modify: `NotchBuddy/NotchBuddy.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` (regenerated)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LinkName.uniqueName`, `LinkWire` (`sizeMax`, `rawNameMax`, `kindMax`).
- Produces: `LinkFileSink(folder:name:quarantine:)` (`open() -> URL`, `write(_:)`, `finish()`, `discard()`, `written`, `defaultFolder()`, `isQuarantined(_:)`, `quarantineByHand`), `LinkFileSource(url:)` (`name`, `size`, `kind`, `read(_:)`, `close()`, `SourceError`).

**Rules.** The sink creates the file **exclusively** (`O_EXCL`), never through a link (`O_NOFOLLOW`), private (0600), and on "already exists" tries the next free name (the volume decides which names are the same file, so `uniqueName` is a first guess, as the contract's known limits say); it makes the file ordinary (0644) only when the transfer is complete; `discard` removes a partial file. Quarantine is set by hand when the file is created (so even a partial file is quarantined) in the GitHub build (`#if APPSTORE` turns it off: the sandbox sets it). The source refuses a folder, a link to a folder, a file over 4 GiB and a name over 1020 bytes, when the user clicks Send.

````swift
import Foundation

// The link's files (LinkFiles.swift): the sink's exclusive create, collisions, the private mode, quarantine and discard, and the source's refusals.
// Run it through scripts/test-link-files.sh. Everything happens in a temp folder.

@main
enum LinkFilesTests {
    static func mode(_ u: URL) -> Int { ((try? FileManager.default.attributesOfItem(atPath: u.path))?[.posixPermissions] as? Int) ?? -1 }

    @MainActor static func main() {
        let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("notchmeow-link-files-\(UUID().uuidString)", isDirectory: true)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: root) }
        let folder = root.appendingPathComponent("Downloads/NotchMeow", isDirectory: true)

        CT.section("the sink")
        let a = LinkFileSink(folder: folder, name: "report.pdf", quarantine: true)
        let ua = try! a.open()
        CT.check("the folder is made and the file takes its name, private while it is written", [ua.lastPathComponent, mode(ua)], ["report.pdf", 0o600] as [AnyHashable])
        try! a.write(Data("hello ".utf8)); try! a.write(Data("world".utf8))
        let done = try! a.finish()
        CT.check("what was written is on disk, and the file is ordinary and quarantined", [try! String(contentsOf: done, encoding: .utf8), mode(done), LinkFileSink.isQuarantined(done) ? 1 : 0] as [AnyHashable], ["hello world", 0o644, 1] as [AnyHashable])
        CT.check("it counted the bytes", a.written, 11)

        let b = LinkFileSink(folder: folder, name: "report.pdf", quarantine: false)
        let ub = try! b.open(); b.discard()
        CT.check("a second file of the same name is numbered, and a discarded one leaves nothing", [ub.lastPathComponent, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: ub.path) ? 1 : 0] as [AnyHashable], ["report (1).pdf", 0] as [AnyHashable])

        let c = LinkFileSink(folder: folder, name: "REPORT.PDF", quarantine: false)
        CT.check("another case of the name is a collision too (the usual volumes compare that way)", (try! c.open()).lastPathComponent, "REPORT (1).PDF")
        c.discard()

        // The volume says a name is taken that the listing did not show: a file made between the listing and the create. The sink must not overwrite it.
        let d = LinkFileSink(folder: folder, name: "race.txt", quarantine: false)
        FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: folder.appendingPathComponent("race.txt").path, contents: Data("mine".utf8))
        let ud = try! d.open()
        CT.check("an existing file is never overwritten", [ud.lastPathComponent, try! String(contentsOf: folder.appendingPathComponent("race.txt"), encoding: .utf8)] as [AnyHashable], ["race (1).txt", "mine"] as [AnyHashable])
        d.discard()

        // A link in the folder with the wanted name: O_NOFOLLOW + O_EXCL refuse to write through it.
        let target = root.appendingPathComponent("elsewhere.txt"); FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: target.path, contents: Data("safe".utf8))
        try! FileManager.default.createSymbolicLink(at: folder.appendingPathComponent("link.txt"), withDestinationURL: target)
        let e = LinkFileSink(folder: folder, name: "link.txt", quarantine: false)
        let ue = try! e.open()
        CT.check("a symbolic link with the wanted name is taken, not followed", [ue.lastPathComponent, try! String(contentsOf: target, encoding: .utf8)] as [AnyHashable], ["link (1).txt", "safe"] as [AnyHashable])
        e.discard()
        CT.checkTrue("the default folder is Downloads/NotchMeow", LinkFileSink.defaultFolder().path.hasSuffix("/Downloads/NotchMeow"))

        CT.section("the source")
        let file = root.appendingPathComponent("send me.txt"); try! Data("0123456789".utf8).write(to: file)
        let s = try! LinkFileSource(url: file)
        CT.check("name, size and a type that is ASCII", [s.name, "\(s.size)", s.kind] as [String], ["send me.txt", "10", "text/plain"])
        CT.check("it reads in pieces and ends with an empty one", [String(decoding: try! s.read(4), as: UTF8.self), String(decoding: try! s.read(4), as: UTF8.self), String(decoding: try! s.read(4), as: UTF8.self), "\(try! s.read(4).count)"], ["0123", "4567", "89", "0"])
        s.close()
        func refused(_ u: URL) -> LinkFileSource.SourceError? { do { _ = try LinkFileSource(url: u); return nil } catch { return error as? LinkFileSource.SourceError } }
        CT.check("a folder is refused", refused(folder), .notAFile)
        CT.check("a link to a folder is refused too", { try! FileManager.default.createSymbolicLink(at: root.appendingPathComponent("dirlink"), withDestinationURL: folder); return refused(root.appendingPathComponent("dirlink")) }(), .notAFile)
        CT.check("a file that is not there is refused", refused(root.appendingPathComponent("nope")), .notAFile)
        let long = root.appendingPathComponent(String(repeating: "a", count: 200) + ".txt"); try! Data().write(to: long)
        CT.check("an empty file is a file (size 0)", try! LinkFileSource(url: long).size, 0)
        CT.finish()
    }
}
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the implementation**

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkFiles.swift`**

````swift
import CoreServices
import Foundation
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

// Files on the link (sub-project G2): where a received file is written and how, and how a file is read to be sent. Foundation only (and the quarantine call).
//
// Receiving. The folder is `~/Downloads/NotchMeow/`. The name is the sanitized one (`LinkName`), made free with `LinkName.uniqueName` and then **created exclusively** (`O_EXCL`): the sanitizer
// compares names the way the usual volumes do, but the volume decides which names are the same file, so on "already exists" the next free name is tried (docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md, known limits).
// The file is never followed through a link (`O_NOFOLLOW`), is created private (0600) and made ordinary (0644) only when it is complete, and is never opened. A transfer that does not
// complete leaves nothing behind (`discard`). On the GitHub build the quarantine attribute is set by hand when the file is created, so that even a partial file is quarantined;
// the App Store build is sandboxed, and the sandbox sets it.

final class LinkFileSink: @unchecked Sendable {
    enum SinkError: Error, Equatable { case noFolder, noFreeName, io(Int32) }

    let folder: URL
    private let wanted: String
    private let quarantine: Bool
    private var fd: Int32 = -1
    private(set) var url: URL?
    private(set) var written: UInt64 = 0

    #if APPSTORE
    static let quarantineByHand = false
    #else
    static let quarantineByHand = true
    #endif

    /// `~/Downloads/NotchMeow/` (the App Store build reaches it with `com.apple.security.files.downloads.read-write`).
    static func defaultFolder() -> URL {
        let downloads = FileManager.default.urls(for: .downloadsDirectory, in: .userDomainMask).first ?? FileManager.default.homeDirectoryForCurrentUser.appendingPathComponent("Downloads")
        return downloads.appendingPathComponent("NotchMeow", isDirectory: true)
    }

    init(folder: URL = LinkFileSink.defaultFolder(), name: String, quarantine: Bool = LinkFileSink.quarantineByHand) {
        self.folder = folder
        self.wanted = name
        self.quarantine = quarantine
    }

    deinit { if fd >= 0 { close(fd) } }

    /// Creates the file under a free name and returns its URL.
    func open() throws -> URL {
        do { try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: folder, withIntermediateDirectories: true) } catch { throw SinkError.noFolder }
        var taken = (try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: folder.path)) ?? []
        for _ in 0..<200 {
            guard let name = LinkName.uniqueName(wanted, taken: taken) else { throw SinkError.noFreeName }
            let target = folder.appendingPathComponent(name)
            let f = Darwin.open(target.path, O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_NOFOLLOW | O_CLOEXEC, 0o600)
            if f >= 0 {
                fd = f
                url = target
                if quarantine { setQuarantine(target) }
                return target
            }
            if errno == EEXIST { taken.append(name); continue }   // the volume says this name is taken: the next free one
            throw SinkError.io(errno)
        }
        throw SinkError.noFreeName
    }

    func write(_ data: Data) throws {
        guard fd >= 0 else { throw SinkError.io(EBADF) }
        var offset = 0
        while offset < data.count {
            let n = data.withUnsafeBytes { Darwin.write(fd, $0.baseAddress! + offset, data.count - offset) }
            if n < 0 { if errno == EINTR { continue }; throw SinkError.io(errno) }
            offset += n
        }
        written += UInt64(data.count)
    }

    /// The transfer is complete: close and make the file ordinary. Returns its URL.
    @discardableResult
    func finish() throws -> URL {
        guard fd >= 0, let url else { throw SinkError.io(EBADF) }
        let rc = close(fd)
        fd = -1
        if rc != 0 { throw SinkError.io(errno) }
        chmod(url.path, 0o644)
        return url
    }

    /// The transfer did not complete: close and remove the partial file.
    func discard() {
        if fd >= 0 { close(fd); fd = -1 }
        if let url { unlink(url.path) }
        url = nil
    }

    private func setQuarantine(_ file: URL) {
        var v = URLResourceValues()
        v.quarantineProperties = [kLSQuarantineAgentNameKey as String: "NotchMeow", kLSQuarantineTypeKey as String: kLSQuarantineTypeOtherDownload as String]
        var f = file
        try? f.setResourceValues(v)
    }

    /// Whether the file carries the quarantine attribute (the tests and the owner's check read it).
    static func isQuarantined(_ file: URL) -> Bool { getxattr(file.path, "com.apple.quarantine", nil, 0, 0, XATTR_NOFOLLOW) >= 0 }
}

/// A file read to be sent: a regular file of at most 4 GiB whose name fits the wire. A folder, a device, a link to either and a file that is too big are refused when the user clicks Send.
final class LinkFileSource: @unchecked Sendable {
    enum SourceError: Error, Equatable { case notAFile, tooLarge, nameTooLong, unreadable }

    let url: URL
    let name: String
    let size: UInt64
    let kind: String
    private var handle: FileHandle?

    init(url: URL) throws {
        let values = try? url.resolvingSymlinksInPath().resourceValues(forKeys: [.isRegularFileKey, .fileSizeKey])
        guard values?.isRegularFile == true, let bytes = values?.fileSize else { throw SourceError.notAFile }
        guard UInt64(bytes) <= LinkWire.sizeMax else { throw SourceError.tooLarge }
        let name = url.lastPathComponent
        guard !name.isEmpty, name.utf8.count <= LinkWire.rawNameMax else { throw SourceError.nameTooLong }
        guard let h = try? FileHandle(forReadingFrom: url) else { throw SourceError.unreadable }
        self.url = url
        self.name = name
        self.size = UInt64(bytes)
        self.handle = h
        // Advisory only, never used to decide anything: the file's MIME type when it has one that is printable ASCII.
        let mime = UTType(filenameExtension: url.pathExtension)?.preferredMIMEType ?? ""
        self.kind = mime.utf8.count <= LinkWire.kindMax && mime.utf8.allSatisfy({ (0x20...0x7E).contains($0) }) ? mime : ""
    }

    deinit { try? handle?.close() }

    /// The next piece, up to `count` bytes; empty at the end of the file.
    func read(_ count: Int) throws -> Data {
        guard let handle else { throw SourceError.unreadable }
        do { return try handle.read(upToCount: count) ?? Data() } catch { throw SourceError.unreadable }
    }

    func close() { try? handle?.close(); handle = nil }
}
````

### Task 6: The conversations: channel, pairing run, transfer runs

**Agent:** `nm-implementer-deep` (Sonnet 5.5, high; **trigger 1**: Swift 6 actors and `AsyncStream` conversations whose ordering, cancellation and hang behaviour the task must get right). Loads `superpowers:systematic-debugging` as well.

**Files:**
- Create: `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkChannel.swift`, `LinkPairRun.swift`, `LinkTransferRun.swift`; `tests/LinkSessionTests.swift`; `scripts/test-link-session.sh`
- Modify: `NotchBuddy/NotchBuddy.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` (regenerated)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1 to 5 (`LinkPairing.Gate`, `LinkHandshake`, `LinkWire`, `LinkPairing`, `LinkFlow`, `LinkFileSink`, `LinkFileSource`, `LinkPeer`, `LinkName`).
- Produces: `LinkIncomingPairing.run(transport:identity:nonce:clock:gate:state:hooks:made:)` (the one function that runs an incoming pairing, behind `LinkPairing.Gate`), `LinkTransport` (`send(_ record:)`, `receive()`, `close()`: the payload of one record, **without** the length prefix, which the real transport adds), `LinkSessionError`, `LinkChannel` (`sendFrame`, `receiveFrame`, `close`), `LinkHandshakeIO`, `LinkMemoryEnd.pair()`, `LinkIdentity`, `LinkPairOutcome`, `LinkPairRun(transport:identity:nonce:clock:hooks:)` (`runInitiator(_:)`, `runResponder(_:)`, `answer(_:)`, `cancel()`, `Hooks{showCode, handshakeDone}`), `LinkOffer`, `LinkTransferOutcome`, `LinkTransferReceive(transport:identity:peer:folder:offerTimeoutMs:hooks:)` (`run()`, `respond(accept:)`, `cancel()`, `Hooks{offered, progress, claim, release, handshakeDone}`), `LinkTransferSend(transport:identity:peer:source:hooks:)` (`run()`, `cancel()`).

**How they work, and why.** Each conversation is one `run()` that consumes an `AsyncStream` of events with a single consumer: the reader task (frames from the peer, then `closed`), the user's click (`answer`, `respond`, `cancel`, all `nonisolated` and thread-safe) and the one-shot timers (the pairing window; an offer's 2 minutes) all post into it, and the pure machines (`LinkPairing`, `LinkFlow`) decide what each event means. `LinkChannel` serializes senders under a lock released in call order, so Noise nonces leave in the order they were taken. Nothing accepts for the user: the receive run turns exactly one thing into an accept event, `respond(accept: true)`. Nothing is written to disk until that click, and a transfer that does not complete discards its partial file. A pairing yields `.paired` only when **both** users said yes; the caller stores the key.

**What the dry run found:** the first draft framed records twice (once by the sender, once by the transport) so the first record never decoded and the initiator waited for ever: the transport's contract is now stated in `LinkTransport`'s comment and the memory end refuses a record outside 1 to 65535 bytes as the real one would. A transfer's "cancel" while an offer waits is **ignored** by the contract (the answer is Decline), and the test pins it. An in-memory transfer finishes before a click can arrive, so the cancel-in-the-middle test paces the sender (`LinkPaced`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test and its script**

The program runs both sides in one process: a pairing with both users agreeing (same six digits on both screens, each side holding the other's name and key), a no on either side, a bit flipped in the reveal and in the answer, a replayed commitment; **an incoming pairing outside the gate** (Devices closed, no click: no run, no card, **nothing sent**, the initiator refused; a click 120 s ago: refused at exactly 120 s; 1 ms earlier: the pairing runs and the card is shown; Devices open: it runs at any time); a transfer of a real 200 000-byte file (SHA-256 equal, quarantined, numbered when the name is taken, progress up to the size), a decline, an offer nobody answers (expired, **no file created**), a busy peer, a cancel click while an offer waits (ignored), a cancel in the middle (both sides cancelled, partial file gone), a stranger's key, and a non-empty first `KK` payload (refused). It has a 90 s watchdog.

**Create `tests/LinkSessionTests.swift`**

````swift
import CryptoKit
import Foundation

// The link's conversations (LinkPairRun, LinkTransferReceive, LinkTransferSend, LinkChannel) between two in-memory ends: a pairing with both users agreeing, a no, a tampered message, a replayed
// commitment; a transfer with a real file, a decline, a cancel, an offer nobody answers, a stranger's key, a non-empty KK payload. No socket, no Keychain, no network.
// Run it through scripts/test-link-session.sh.

actor Counter { var n = 0; func add() { n += 1 }; func value() -> Int { n } }
actor Box<T: Sendable> { var v: T?; func set(_ x: T) { v = x }; func get() -> T? { v }; var isSet: Bool { v != nil } }

/// Counts what an end sends, to prove that a refused connection is answered with nothing.
actor LinkCounting: LinkTransport {
    let inner: any LinkTransport
    var sent = 0
    init(_ inner: any LinkTransport) { self.inner = inner }
    func send(_ record: Data) async throws { sent += 1; try await inner.send(record) }
    func receive() async throws -> Data { try await inner.receive() }
    func close() async { await inner.close() }
}

/// Flips one bit of the `index`th record sent through it (0 is the preamble).
actor LinkTamper: LinkTransport {
    let inner: any LinkTransport
    let index: Int
    var count = 0
    init(_ inner: any LinkTransport, flipRecord index: Int) { self.inner = inner; self.index = index }
    func send(_ record: Data) async throws {
        var r = record
        if count == index { r[r.count - 1] ^= 1 }
        count += 1
        try await inner.send(r)
    }
    func receive() async throws -> Data { try await inner.receive() }
    func close() async { await inner.close() }
}

/// Polls a condition every 5 ms for up to 2 s (a test helper: the app itself never polls).
func waitUntil(_ condition: @Sendable () async -> Bool) async {
    for _ in 0..<400 { if await condition() { return }; try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(5)) }
}

/// Waits a few milliseconds before every send, so that a transfer is still running when the test's click arrives.
actor LinkPaced: LinkTransport {
    let inner: any LinkTransport
    init(_ inner: any LinkTransport) { self.inner = inner }
    func send(_ record: Data) async throws { try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(3)); try await inner.send(record) }
    func receive() async throws -> Data { try await inner.receive() }
    func close() async { await inner.close() }
}

@main
enum LinkSessionTests {
    static func identity(_ n: UInt8, _ name: String) -> LinkIdentity {
        LinkIdentity(deviceId: String(repeating: String(format: "%02x", n), count: 16), name: name, key: try! LinkNoise.privateKey(raw: Data(repeating: n, count: 32)))
    }
    static let nonceA = Data(repeating: 0xA1, count: 32), nonceB = Data(repeating: 0xB1, count: 32)
    static func now(_ ms: Int) -> @Sendable () -> Int { { ms } }

    @MainActor static func main() async {
        setvbuf(stdout, nil, _IONBF, 0)   // a hang then shows how far it got
        DispatchQueue.global().asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 90) { print("timed out: a conversation never ended"); exit(1) }   // a test, not the app: a hang must fail the run
        let mac = identity(0x11, "Studio Mac"), pc = identity(0x22, "Desk PC")
        let macPub = LinkBytes.hex(LinkNoise.publicKey(of: mac.key)), pcPub = LinkBytes.hex(LinkNoise.publicKey(of: pc.key))

        // MARK: pairing
        func pair(initiatorSays: Bool?, responderSays: Bool?, tamperRecord: Int? = nil, responderState: LinkPairing.ResponderState = .init()) async -> (LinkPairOutcome, LinkPairOutcome, String?, String?) {
            let (a, b) = await LinkMemoryEnd.pair()
            let ta: any LinkTransport = tamperRecord.map { LinkTamper(a, flipRecord: $0) } ?? a
            let codeA = Box<String>(), codeB = Box<String>()
            let initiator = LinkPairRun(transport: ta, identity: mac, nonce: nonceA, clock: now(1000), hooks: .init(showCode: { c, _ in Task { await codeA.set(c) } }))
            let responder = LinkPairRun(transport: b, identity: pc, nonce: nonceB, clock: now(1000), hooks: .init(showCode: { c, _ in Task { await codeB.set(c) } }))
            // The preamble is read by the listener in the app: here the test reads and checks it.
            async let ri = initiator.runInitiator(.init())
            async let rr: (LinkPairOutcome, LinkPairing.ResponderState) = {
                let pre = try? LinkWire.decodePreamble(try await b.receive())
                guard pre?.mode == .pair else { return (.refused("no preamble"), responderState) }
                return await responder.runResponder(responderState)
            }()
            // The users answer once the codes are on screen.
            Task {
                await waitUntil { await codeA.isSet }
                await waitUntil { await codeB.isSet }
                if let y = initiatorSays { initiator.answer(y) }
                if let y = responderSays { responder.answer(y) }
            }
            let (oi, _) = await ri, (or, _) = await rr
            return (oi, or, await codeA.get(), await codeB.get())
        }

        CT.section("pairing: both users say they match")
        let ok = await pair(initiatorSays: true, responderSays: true)
        CT.check("both sides are paired with each other", [ok.0, ok.1].map { o -> String in if case .paired(let p) = o { return "\(p.name) \(p.key.prefix(8))" }; return "no" }, ["Desk PC \(pcPub.prefix(8))", "Studio Mac \(macPub.prefix(8))"])
        CT.check("the two screens show the same six digits", [ok.2 == ok.3, ok.2?.count == 6], [true, true])

        CT.section("pairing: a no on either side stores nothing")
        let no = await pair(initiatorSays: true, responderSays: false)
        CT.check("the responder said no: both see it", [no.0, no.1], [.declined(byMe: false), .declined(byMe: true)])
        let no2 = await pair(initiatorSays: false, responderSays: nil)
        CT.check("the initiator said no first", [no2.0, no2.1], [.declined(byMe: true), .declined(byMe: false)])

        CT.section("pairing: damaged and replayed")
        func isPaired(_ o: LinkPairOutcome) -> Bool { if case .paired = o { return true }; return false }
        let bad = await pair(initiatorSays: true, responderSays: true, tamperRecord: 2)
        CT.check("a bit flipped in the initiator's reveal (the last handshake message): neither side is paired", [isPaired(bad.0), isPaired(bad.1)], [false, false])
        let bad2 = await pair(initiatorSays: true, responderSays: true, tamperRecord: 3)
        CT.check("a bit flipped in the initiator's answer: the responder, which could not open it, stores nothing", isPaired(bad2.1), false)
        CT.check("a commitment seen again inside the window is refused as a replay", await {
            var seen = LinkPairing.ResponderState()
            seen.seen = [.init(commit: LinkBytes.hex(LinkPairing.commitOf(nonce: nonceA, staticKey: LinkNoise.publicKey(of: mac.key))!), at: 0)]
            seen.last = nil
            return await pair(initiatorSays: nil, responderSays: nil, responderState: seen).1
        }(), .refused("commit-replayed"))

        // MARK: the gate on an incoming pairing
        func incoming(gate: LinkPairing.Gate, at ms: Int) async -> (initiator: LinkPairOutcome, responder: LinkPairOutcome?, cardShown: Bool, sentByResponder: Bool) {
            let (a, b) = await LinkMemoryEnd.pair()
            let counted = LinkCounting(b)
            let card = Box<String>(), made = Box<LinkPairRun>()
            let initiator = LinkPairRun(transport: a, identity: mac, nonce: nonceA, clock: now(ms), hooks: .init(showCode: { _, _ in }))
            async let ri = initiator.runInitiator(.init())
            async let rr: LinkPairOutcome? = {
                guard (try? LinkWire.decodePreamble(try await counted.receive()))?.mode == .pair else { return nil }
                return await LinkIncomingPairing.run(transport: counted, identity: pc, nonce: nonceB, clock: now(ms), gate: gate, state: .init(), hooks: .init(showCode: { c, _ in Task { await card.set(c) } }), made: { r in Task { await made.set(r) } })?.0
            }()
            // When the card is up nobody answers in a test: both users cancel, which ends the pairing as a no. A refused connection never shows a card, so this waits out its 2 s and does nothing.
            Task { await waitUntil { await card.isSet }; if await card.isSet { initiator.cancel(); await made.get()?.cancel() } }
            let (oi, _) = await ri
            let r = await rr
            return (oi, r, await card.isSet, await counted.sent > 0)
        }
        var closedGate = LinkPairing.Gate()
        CT.section("an incoming pairing outside the gate is refused silently")
        let outside = await incoming(gate: closedGate, at: 1000)
        CT.check("Devices closed and no click: no run, no card, nothing sent, and the initiator is refused", [outside.responder == nil, outside.cardShown, outside.sentByResponder, outside.initiator == .refused("handshake")], [true, false, false, true])
        closedGate.pairClicked(now: 0)
        let atEdge = await incoming(gate: closedGate, at: 120_000)
        CT.check("a click 120 s ago is not enough: refused at exactly 120 s, no card", [atEdge.responder == nil, atEdge.cardShown], [true, false])
        let insideClick = await incoming(gate: closedGate, at: 119_999)
        CT.check("1 ms earlier it is inside the window: the pairing runs and the card is shown", [insideClick.responder != nil, insideClick.cardShown], [true, true])
        var open = LinkPairing.Gate()
        open.devicesAppeared()
        let withDevices = await incoming(gate: open, at: 500_000)
        CT.check("with Devices open it runs at any time", [withDevices.responder != nil, withDevices.cardShown], [true, true])

        // MARK: transfer
        let root = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("notchmeow-link-session-\(UUID().uuidString)", isDirectory: true)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: root) }
        try! FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: root, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let macPeer = LinkPeer(id: mac.deviceId, name: mac.name, key: macPub, at: 1), pcPeer = LinkPeer(id: pc.deviceId, name: pc.name, key: pcPub, at: 1)

        func transfer(file: URL, folder: URL, respond: Bool?, cancelClickBeforeAnswer: Bool = false, offerTimeoutMs: Int = 120_000, senderPeer: LinkPeer? = nil, claim: Bool = true, receiverCancelsAtBytes: UInt64? = nil, paced: Bool = false)
            async -> (sender: LinkTransferOutcome, receiver: LinkTransferOutcome, offer: LinkOffer?, progress: [UInt64]) {
            let (a, b) = await LinkMemoryEnd.pair()
            let offered = Box<LinkOffer>(), prog = Box<[UInt64]>(), who = Box<LinkTransferReceive>()
            await prog.set([])
            let receiver = LinkTransferReceive(transport: b, identity: pc, peer: macPeer, folder: folder, offerTimeoutMs: offerTimeoutMs, hooks: .init(
                offered: { o in Task { await offered.set(o) } },
                progress: { got, _ in Task { var p = await prog.get() ?? []; p.append(got); await prog.set(p); if let limit = receiverCancelsAtBytes, got >= limit { await who.get()?.cancel() } } },
                claim: { _ in claim }, release: { _ in }))
            await who.set(receiver)
            let sender = LinkTransferSend(transport: paced ? LinkPaced(a) : a, identity: mac, peer: senderPeer ?? pcPeer, source: try! LinkFileSource(url: file), hooks: .init(progress: { _, _ in }))
            Task {
                await waitUntil { await offered.isSet }
                if cancelClickBeforeAnswer { receiver.cancel() }      // a cancel click while an offer waits is ignored: the answer is Decline
                if let r = respond { receiver.respond(accept: r) }
            }
            async let rs = sender.run()
            async let rr: LinkTransferOutcome = {
                let pre = try? LinkWire.decodePreamble(try await b.receive())
                guard pre?.mode == .transfer, pre?.id == mac.deviceId else { return .aborted("no preamble") }
                return await receiver.run()
            }()
            let (s, r) = (await rs, await rr)
            return (s, r, await offered.get(), await prog.get() ?? [])
        }

        let folder = root.appendingPathComponent("Downloads/NotchMeow", isDirectory: true)
        let big = root.appendingPathComponent("photo.bin")
        var payload = Data(count: 200_000); for i in 0..<payload.count { payload[i] = UInt8(truncatingIfNeeded: i &* 31 &+ 7) }
        try! payload.write(to: big)

        CT.section("transfer: a file of 200 000 bytes, accepted")
        let t1 = await transfer(file: big, folder: folder, respond: true)
        var received: URL?; if case .completed(let u) = t1.receiver { received = u }
        CT.check("the receiver completed and the sender finished", [t1.receiver == .completed(received), t1.sender == .completed(nil)], [true, true])
        CT.check("the card named the file and its size, and the peer", [t1.offer?.name, t1.offer.map { "\($0.size)" }, t1.offer?.peerName] as [String?], ["photo.bin", "200000", "Studio Mac"])
        CT.check("the bytes are the same (SHA-256)", received.map { SHA256.hash(data: try! Data(contentsOf: $0)).description } , SHA256.hash(data: payload).description)
        CT.check("it lives in the folder, quarantined, and progress went up to the size", [received?.deletingLastPathComponent().path == folder.path, received.map(LinkFileSink.isQuarantined) == true, t1.progress.last == 200_000], [true, true, true])

        CT.section("transfer: a second file with the same name is numbered")
        let t2 = await transfer(file: big, folder: folder, respond: true)
        if case .completed(let u2?) = t2.receiver { CT.check("photo (1).bin", u2.lastPathComponent, "photo (1).bin") } else { CT.checkTrue("the second transfer completed", false) }

        CT.section("transfer: declined, not answered, cancelled, busy")
        let before = (try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: folder.path).count) ?? 0
        let t3 = await transfer(file: big, folder: folder, respond: false)
        CT.check("a decline reaches the sender with reason 0 and creates no file", [t3.sender, t3.receiver, .completed(nil)] as [LinkTransferOutcome], [.declined(reason: 0), .declined(reason: 0), .completed(nil)])
        CT.check("the folder has no new file", (try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: folder.path).count) ?? 0, before)
        let t4 = await transfer(file: big, folder: folder, respond: nil, offerTimeoutMs: 60)
        CT.check("nobody answers: the offer expires, the sender sees reason 1 (timeout), and nothing was written", [t4.receiver, t4.sender, .completed(nil)] as [LinkTransferOutcome], [.expired, .declined(reason: 1), .completed(nil)])
        CT.check("still no new file: nothing accepted by itself", (try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: folder.path).count) ?? 0, before)
        let t5 = await transfer(file: big, folder: folder, respond: true, claim: false)
        CT.check("another transfer of the same peer is running: declined as busy (reason 2)", t5.sender, .declined(reason: 2))
        let t6 = await transfer(file: big, folder: folder, respond: false, cancelClickBeforeAnswer: true)
        CT.check("a cancel click while an offer waits is ignored; the Decline that follows is the answer", t6.sender, .declined(reason: 0))
        let t8 = await transfer(file: big, folder: folder, respond: true, receiverCancelsAtBytes: 65_536, paced: true)
        CT.check("the receiver's user cancels in the middle: both sides end cancelled", [t8.sender, t8.receiver], [.cancelled, .cancelled])
        CT.check("and the partial file is gone", (try? FileManager.default.contentsOfDirectory(atPath: folder.path).count) ?? 0, before)

        CT.section("transfer: a stranger's key, and a non-empty KK payload")
        let stranger = LinkPeer(id: pc.deviceId, name: "x", key: LinkBytes.hex(LinkNoise.publicKey(of: identity(0x33, "x").key)), at: 1)
        let t7 = await transfer(file: big, folder: folder, respond: true, senderPeer: stranger)
        CT.check("a sender that holds another key for the receiver cannot complete the handshake", [t7.sender, t7.receiver], [.aborted("handshake"), .aborted("handshake")])
        // A peer whose first KK payload is not empty: the receiver refuses it.
        let (x, y) = await LinkMemoryEnd.pair()
        let strict = LinkTransferReceive(transport: y, identity: pc, peer: macPeer, folder: folder, hooks: .init(offered: { _ in }, progress: { _, _ in }, claim: { _ in true }, release: { _ in }))
        async let strictRun = strict.run()
        var hs = try! LinkHandshake(pattern: .kk, initiator: true, s: mac.key, rs: LinkBytes.data(hex: pcPub)!)
        try! await LinkHandshakeIO.send(x, try! hs.write(Data("hi".utf8)))
        CT.check("a non-empty payload in the first KK message is refused", await strictRun, .aborted("handshake"))
        await x.close()
        CT.finish()
    }
}
````
