- [ ] **Step 2: Write the implementation**

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkChannel.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation

// The link's connection layer (sub-project G2): a transport that carries whole records, and the channel that puts Noise transport messages and frames on it. Foundation only, so the session
// tests can run it between two in-memory ends. The real transport (Network.framework, LinkNetwork.swift) cuts a TCP stream into records with `LinkWire.splitRecords`; this file does not know
// sockets.

/// Carries whole records (one record is one Noise message, or the preamble): `send` takes a record's bytes, 1 to 65535 of them, and `receive` gives the bytes of the next whole record, without the
/// length prefix that the wire puts in front (`LinkWire.encodeRecord`, which the real transport applies). Every call may suspend; `receive` throws when the other end has closed.
protocol LinkTransport: Sendable {
    func send(_ record: Data) async throws
    func receive() async throws -> Data
    /// Flushes what was sent and closes. Calling it twice is harmless.
    func close() async
}

enum LinkSessionError: Error, Equatable, Sendable {
    case closed
    case violation(String)   // the other side broke the protocol, or a message did not open
    case io(String)
}

/// Frames over an established Noise session. One sender at a time: a frame is encrypted and handed to the transport under a lock that is released in call order, so the nonces go out in the
/// order they were taken (a second sender that arrives while the first is waiting for the transport waits its turn).
actor LinkChannel {
    private let transport: any LinkTransport
    private var sendState: LinkCipherState
    private var receiveState: LinkCipherState
    private var busy = false
    private var waiters: [CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>] = []

    init(transport: any LinkTransport, send: LinkCipherState, receive: LinkCipherState) {
        self.transport = transport
        self.sendState = send
        self.receiveState = receive
    }

    private func lock() async {
        if busy { await withCheckedContinuation { waiters.append($0) } } else { busy = true }
    }
    private func unlock() {
        if waiters.isEmpty { busy = false } else { waiters.removeFirst().resume() }
    }

    func sendFrame(_ frame: LinkWire.Frame) async throws {
        await lock()
        defer { unlock() }
        do {
            let sealed = try sendState.encrypt(ad: Data(), try LinkWire.encode(frame))
            try await transport.send(sealed)
        } catch let e as LinkSessionError { throw e }
        catch let e as LinkNoiseError { throw LinkSessionError.violation("noise \(e)") }
        catch let e as LinkFrameError { throw LinkSessionError.violation("frame \(e.rawValue)") }
        catch { throw LinkSessionError.io("\(error)") }
    }

    /// The next frame. A message that does not open, a frame that does not decode and a closed transport all end the conversation with an error.
    func receiveFrame() async throws -> LinkWire.Frame {
        let record: Data
        do { record = try await transport.receive() } catch let e as LinkSessionError { throw e } catch { throw LinkSessionError.io("\(error)") }
        do { return try LinkWire.decode(try receiveState.decrypt(ad: Data(), record)) }
        catch let e as LinkNoiseError { throw LinkSessionError.violation("noise \(e)") }
        catch let e as LinkFrameError { throw LinkSessionError.violation("frame \(e.rawValue)") }
    }

    func close() async { await transport.close() }
}

/// Handshake messages over a transport, one record each. `Data` that comes back from `receive` is re-based at 0.
enum LinkHandshakeIO {
    static func send(_ transport: any LinkTransport, _ message: Data) async throws {
        try await transport.send(message)
    }
    static func receive(_ transport: any LinkTransport) async throws -> Data { Data(try await transport.receive()) }
}

/// Two ends of an in-memory connection, for the tests and for the Debug fake peer's loopback (no sockets, no network). Records come out in the order they went in; after `close` the other end
/// reads what was already sent and then sees the end.
actor LinkMemoryEnd: LinkTransport {
    private var queue: [Data] = []
    private var waiter: CheckedContinuation<Data?, Never>?
    private var peer: LinkMemoryEnd?
    private var closed = false      // this end closed
    private var ended = false       // nothing more will arrive

    static func pair() async -> (LinkMemoryEnd, LinkMemoryEnd) {
        let a = LinkMemoryEnd(), b = LinkMemoryEnd()
        await a.link(to: b)
        await b.link(to: a)
        return (a, b)
    }

    private func link(to other: LinkMemoryEnd) { peer = other }

    private func deliver(_ record: Data) {
        if let w = waiter { waiter = nil; w.resume(returning: record) } else { queue.append(record) }
    }

    private func peerClosed() {
        ended = true
        if let w = waiter { waiter = nil; w.resume(returning: nil) }
    }

    func send(_ record: Data) async throws {
        guard (1...LinkWire.noiseMax).contains(record.count) else { throw LinkSessionError.violation("record length") }   // what the real transport's length prefix would refuse
        guard !closed, !ended, let peer else { throw LinkSessionError.closed }
        await peer.deliver(Data(record))
    }

    func receive() async throws -> Data {
        if !queue.isEmpty { return queue.removeFirst() }
        if ended { throw LinkSessionError.closed }
        guard let record = await withCheckedContinuation({ (c: CheckedContinuation<Data?, Never>) in waiter = c }) else { throw LinkSessionError.closed }
        return record
    }

    func close() async {
        guard !closed else { return }
        closed = true
        ended = true
        if let w = waiter { waiter = nil; w.resume(returning: nil) }
        await peer?.peerClosed()
    }
}
````

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkPairRun.swift`**

````swift
import CryptoKit
import Foundation

// One pairing conversation (sub-project G2, docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md "The pairing code"), on either side. The rules are the pure machines of LinkPairing, run one event at a time; this file only
// does the I/O around them: the preamble, the three `XX` handshake messages (commitment, hello with the responder's nonce, hello with the initiator's reveal), the code, the user's answer, the
// other side's answer, and the two minutes of the window. Nothing is stored here: a `.paired` outcome says that **both users said yes**, and the caller writes the key (LinkKeychain.add
// re-checks the paired map at that moment).
//
// Nothing here reads a clock or draws a random number of its own: `clock` (whole milliseconds, monotonic) and the nonce come in, so a test can run both sides in one process.

struct LinkIdentity: Sendable {
    let deviceId: String                                  // 32 lower-case hex digits
    let name: String                                      // shown on the other machine's card
    let key: Curve25519.KeyAgreement.PrivateKey           // the static key (LinkKeychain.staticKey)
}

enum LinkPairOutcome: Equatable, Sendable {
    case paired(LinkPeer)
    /// A user said no: this machine's (`byMe`) or the other's.
    case declined(byMe: Bool)
    /// A rule or the wire refused: a `LinkPairing.Refusal` raw value, or "closed", "handshake", "unexpected-frame".
    case refused(String)
}

/// An incoming pairing connection, behind the gate: when the gate is closed nothing is sent and the connection is closed, as for any other refused hello, and nobody sees a card. The service
/// uses this one function for every incoming pairing, so the rule is tested where it is applied.
enum LinkIncomingPairing {
    /// nil when refused. `made` is called with the run before it starts, so the caller can answer it.
    static func run(transport: any LinkTransport, identity: LinkIdentity, nonce: Data, clock: @escaping @Sendable () -> Int, gate: LinkPairing.Gate, state: LinkPairing.ResponderState,
                    hooks: LinkPairRun.Hooks, made: @Sendable (LinkPairRun) -> Void = { _ in }) async -> (LinkPairOutcome, LinkPairing.ResponderState)? {
        guard gate.allows(now: clock()) else { await transport.close(); return nil }
        let run = LinkPairRun(transport: transport, identity: identity, nonce: nonce, clock: clock, hooks: hooks)
        made(run)
        return await run.runResponder(state)
    }
}

actor LinkPairRun {
    struct Hooks: Sendable {
        /// The code is known: show it, with the other machine's authenticated name.
        var showCode: @Sendable (_ code: String, _ peerName: String) -> Void
        /// The handshake is over (the other side is authenticated); the connection no longer needs the listener's watchdog.
        var handshakeDone: @Sendable () -> Void = {}
    }

    private enum Event: Sendable { case frame(LinkWire.Frame), closed, user(Bool), windowEnded }

    private let transport: any LinkTransport
    private let identity: LinkIdentity
    private let nonce: Data
    private let clock: @Sendable () -> Int
    private let hooks: Hooks
    private let events: AsyncStream<Event>
    private let post: AsyncStream<Event>.Continuation

    /// `nonce` is this side's fresh 32 random bytes (the commitment's, or the responder's answer).
    init(transport: any LinkTransport, identity: LinkIdentity, nonce: Data, clock: @escaping @Sendable () -> Int, hooks: Hooks) {
        self.transport = transport
        self.identity = identity
        self.nonce = nonce
        self.clock = clock
        self.hooks = hooks
        (events, post) = AsyncStream<Event>.makeStream()
    }

    /// The user's click on "They match" (true) or "No" (false).
    nonisolated func answer(_ yes: Bool) { post.yield(.user(yes)) }
    /// The user closed the card: a no, and the conversation ends.
    nonisolated func cancel() { post.yield(.user(false)) }

    private struct Stepped { var notify: LinkPairing.Notify?; var send: [LinkPairing.Send]; var because: LinkPairing.Refusal? }

    // MARK: Initiator

    func runInitiator(_ start: LinkPairing.InitiatorState) async -> (LinkPairOutcome, LinkPairing.InitiatorState) {
        var st = start
        do {
            var hs = try LinkHandshake(pattern: .xx, initiator: true, s: identity.key)
            let myStatic = LinkBytes.hex(LinkNoise.publicKey(of: identity.key))
            let o = LinkPairing.initiatorStep(st, .start(nonce: LinkBytes.hex(nonce), staticKey: myStatic, now: clock()))
            st = o.state
            if let why = o.because { await transport.close(); return (.refused(why.rawValue), st) }
            guard case .commit(let commitHex)? = o.send.first, let commit = LinkBytes.data(hex: commitHex) else { await transport.close(); return (.refused("handshake"), st) }
            try await transport.send(LinkWire.encodePreamble(mode: .pair, deviceId: Data(count: LinkWire.idLen)))
            try await LinkHandshakeIO.send(transport, try hs.write(commit))
            let hello = try LinkWire.decodeHello(try hs.read(try await LinkHandshakeIO.receive(transport)))
            guard let remote = hs.remoteStatic else { throw LinkSessionError.violation("no remote key") }
            let o2 = LinkPairing.initiatorStep(st, .nonce(hello.nonce, peerStatic: LinkBytes.hex(remote), peerId: hello.id, now: clock()))
            st = o2.state
            if let why = o2.because { await transport.close(); return (.refused(why.rawValue), st) }
            let reveal = LinkWire.encodeHello(deviceId: LinkBytes.data(hex: identity.deviceId)!, nonce: nonce, name: identity.name)
            try await LinkHandshakeIO.send(transport, try hs.write(reveal))
            let (_, send, receive) = try hs.finish()
            guard case .coded(let startedAt, let code, _, _) = st.phase else { throw LinkSessionError.violation("no code") }
            let peer = LinkPeer(id: hello.id, name: hello.name, key: LinkBytes.hex(remote), at: Int(Date().timeIntervalSince1970))
            hooks.handshakeDone()
            hooks.showCode(code, hello.name)
            let channel = LinkChannel(transport: transport, send: send, receive: receive)
            let outcome = await answers(channel, windowStart: startedAt, peer: peer) { event in
                switch event {
                case .user(let yes, let now): let r = LinkPairing.initiatorStep(st, .user(yes: yes, now: now)); st = r.state; return Stepped(notify: r.notify, send: r.send, because: r.because)
                case .peer(let yes, let now): let r = LinkPairing.initiatorStep(st, .peer(yes: yes, now: now)); st = r.state; return Stepped(notify: r.notify, send: r.send, because: r.because)
                }
            }
            await transport.close()
            return (outcome, st)
        } catch {
            await transport.close()
            st.phase = .idle
            return (.refused("handshake"), st)
        }
    }

    // MARK: Responder

    /// The preamble was read already (the listener reads it to tell a pairing from a transfer).
    func runResponder(_ start: LinkPairing.ResponderState) async -> (LinkPairOutcome, LinkPairing.ResponderState) {
        var st = start
        do {
            var hs = try LinkHandshake(pattern: .xx, initiator: false, s: identity.key)
            let commit = try hs.read(try await LinkHandshakeIO.receive(transport))
            let o = LinkPairing.responderStep(st, .commit(commit: LinkBytes.hex(commit), nonce: LinkBytes.hex(nonce), now: clock()))
            st = o.state
            if let why = o.because { await transport.close(); return (.refused(why.rawValue), st) }
            let hello = LinkWire.encodeHello(deviceId: LinkBytes.data(hex: identity.deviceId)!, nonce: nonce, name: identity.name)
            try await LinkHandshakeIO.send(transport, try hs.write(hello))
            let reveal = try LinkWire.decodeHello(try hs.read(try await LinkHandshakeIO.receive(transport)))
            guard let remote = hs.remoteStatic else { throw LinkSessionError.violation("no remote key") }
            let myStatic = LinkBytes.hex(LinkNoise.publicKey(of: identity.key))
            let o2 = LinkPairing.responderStep(st, .reveal(LinkPairing.Reveal(nonce: reveal.nonce, staticKey: LinkBytes.hex(remote), own: myStatic, peerId: reveal.id), now: clock()))
            st = o2.state
            if let why = o2.because { await transport.close(); return (.refused(why.rawValue), st) }
            let (_, send, receive) = try hs.finish()
            guard case .coded(let startedAt, let code, _, _) = st.phase else { throw LinkSessionError.violation("no code") }
            let peer = LinkPeer(id: reveal.id, name: reveal.name, key: LinkBytes.hex(remote), at: Int(Date().timeIntervalSince1970))
            hooks.handshakeDone()
            hooks.showCode(code, reveal.name)
            let channel = LinkChannel(transport: transport, send: send, receive: receive)
            let outcome = await answers(channel, windowStart: startedAt, peer: peer) { event in
                switch event {
                case .user(let yes, let now): let r = LinkPairing.responderStep(st, .user(yes: yes, now: now)); st = r.state; return Stepped(notify: r.notify, send: r.send, because: r.because)
                case .peer(let yes, let now): let r = LinkPairing.responderStep(st, .peer(yes: yes, now: now)); st = r.state; return Stepped(notify: r.notify, send: r.send, because: r.because)
                }
            }
            await transport.close()
            return (outcome, st)
        } catch {
            await transport.close()
            st.phase = .idle
            return (.refused("handshake"), st)
        }
    }

    // MARK: The answers (both roles)

    private enum Answer { case user(Bool, now: Int), peer(Bool, now: Int) }

    /// After the code is shown: the user's click and the other side's `pair-yes` or `pair-no`, in either order, inside the window. The only frames a pairing connection may carry.
    private func answers(_ channel: LinkChannel, windowStart: Int, peer: LinkPeer, step: (Answer) -> Stepped) async -> LinkPairOutcome {
        let post = self.post
        let reader = Task {
            do { while true { post.yield(.frame(try await channel.receiveFrame())) } } catch { post.yield(.closed) }
        }
        let remaining = max(0, LinkPairing.windowMs - (clock() - windowStart))
        let timer = Task {
            try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(remaining))
            if !Task.isCancelled { post.yield(.windowEnded) }
        }
        defer { reader.cancel(); timer.cancel() }

        for await event in events {
            var stepped: Stepped
            var iAnswered = false
            switch event {
            case .user(let yes): stepped = step(.user(yes, now: clock())); iAnswered = true
            case .frame(.pairYes): stepped = step(.peer(true, now: clock()))
            case .frame(.pairNo): stepped = step(.peer(false, now: clock()))
            case .frame: return .refused("unexpected-frame")
            case .closed: return .refused("closed")
            case .windowEnded: return .refused(LinkPairing.Refusal.windowClosed.rawValue)
            }
            for s in stepped.send {
                if case .pair(let yes) = s { try? await channel.sendFrame(yes ? .pairYes : .pairNo) }
            }
            if stepped.notify == .store { return .paired(peer) }
            if stepped.notify == .discard {
                if stepped.because == .saidNo { return .declined(byMe: iAnswered) }
                return .refused(stepped.because?.rawValue ?? "discard")
            }
            stepped.notify = nil
        }
        return .refused("closed")
    }
}
````

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkTransferRun.swift`**

````swift
import CryptoKit
import Foundation

// One transfer conversation (sub-project G2, docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md "What each side does with each frame"), on either side. The rules are the pure machines of LinkFlow, run one event at a time;
// this file does the I/O around them: the preamble and the two `KK` handshake messages, the offer, the user's answer, the chunks to or from disk, progress, cancel, and the two minutes an offer
// may wait. Only a click on Accept accepts an offer (`respond(accept:)` is called from that button alone): nothing in this file accepts for the user.
//
// A transfer runs only between paired machines: the sender knows the receiver's stored key, and the receiver looks up the sender's device id from the preamble and refuses one it does not know
// (the caller does that before it makes a `LinkTransferReceive`). Nothing here reads a clock or draws a random number except the transfer id the sender makes.

enum LinkTransferOutcome: Equatable, Sendable {
    case completed(URL?)               // the received file's URL; nil on the sending side
    case declined(reason: UInt8)       // the receiver said no (sender), or this side's own decline (receiver: reason 0)
    case expired                       // nobody answered the offer in 2 minutes
    case cancelled                     // either user cancelled during the transfer, or the other side did
    case withdrawn                     // the sender withdrew an offer that was waiting
    case aborted(String)               // a rule or the wire ended it: a `LinkFlow.Abort` raw value, or "closed", "handshake", "disk"
}

/// What the Accept card shows. `name` is the **sanitized** name, the one that will be saved.
struct LinkOffer: Equatable, Sendable {
    let transferId: String
    let name: String
    let size: UInt64
    let peerId: String
    let peerName: String
}

// MARK: - Receiving

actor LinkTransferReceive {
    struct Hooks: Sendable {
        /// An offer is waiting for a click. Show the card.
        var offered: @Sendable (LinkOffer) -> Void
        var progress: @Sendable (_ received: UInt64, _ total: UInt64) -> Void
        /// One active transfer per peer: false when another connection of this peer is already transferring (the offer is then declined as busy).
        var claim: @Sendable (_ peerId: String) -> Bool
        var release: @Sendable (_ peerId: String) -> Void
        /// The handshake is over; the connection no longer needs the listener's watchdog.
        var handshakeDone: @Sendable () -> Void = {}
    }

    private enum Event: Sendable { case frame(LinkWire.Frame), closed, flow(LinkFlow.Event) }

    private let transport: any LinkTransport
    private let identity: LinkIdentity
    private let peer: LinkPeer
    private let folder: URL
    private let hooks: Hooks
    private let offerTimeoutMs: Int
    private let events: AsyncStream<Event>
    private let post: AsyncStream<Event>.Continuation

    init(transport: any LinkTransport, identity: LinkIdentity, peer: LinkPeer, folder: URL = LinkFileSink.defaultFolder(), offerTimeoutMs: Int = LinkPairing.offerTimeoutMs, hooks: Hooks) {
        self.transport = transport
        self.identity = identity
        self.peer = peer
        self.folder = folder
        self.offerTimeoutMs = offerTimeoutMs
        self.hooks = hooks
        (events, post) = AsyncStream<Event>.makeStream()
    }

    /// The user's click on Accept (true) or Decline (false).
    nonisolated func respond(accept: Bool) { post.yield(.flow(.user(accept ? .accept : .decline))) }
    /// The user cancels the transfer that is running (or declines the offer that is waiting).
    nonisolated func cancel() { post.yield(.flow(.user(.cancel))) }

    /// The preamble was read already. Runs the `KK` handshake, then the conversation.
    func run() async -> LinkTransferOutcome {
        var claimed = false
        var sink: LinkFileSink?
        func finish(_ o: LinkTransferOutcome) async -> LinkTransferOutcome {
            if case .completed = o {} else { sink?.discard() }
            if claimed { hooks.release(peer.id) }
            await transport.close()
            return o
        }
        let channel: LinkChannel
        do {
            guard let remote = LinkBytes.data(hex: peer.key) else { return await finish(.aborted("handshake")) }
            var hs = try LinkHandshake(pattern: .kk, initiator: false, s: identity.key, rs: remote)
            let first = try hs.read(try await LinkHandshakeIO.receive(transport))
            guard first.isEmpty else { return await finish(.aborted("handshake")) }       // the KK payloads are empty (docs/LINK-CONTRACT.md); a port refuses a non-empty one
            try await LinkHandshakeIO.send(transport, try hs.write(Data()))
            let (_, send, receive) = try hs.finish()
            channel = LinkChannel(transport: transport, send: send, receive: receive)
            hooks.handshakeDone()
        } catch { return await finish(.aborted("handshake")) }

        let post = self.post
        let reader = Task { do { while true { post.yield(.frame(try await channel.receiveFrame())) } } catch { post.yield(.closed) } }
        var timer: Task<Void, Never>?
        defer { reader.cancel(); timer?.cancel() }

        var state = LinkFlow.ReceiverState.idle
        var total: UInt64 = 0
        var offeredName = ""
        for await event in events {
            let flowEvent: LinkFlow.Event
            switch event {
            case .closed: return await finish(.aborted("closed"))
            case .frame(let f): flowEvent = .frame(f)
            case .flow(let e): flowEvent = e
            }
            // Busy across connections: the machine models this connection only.
            if case .frame(.offer(let id, _, _, _)) = flowEvent, case .idle = state, !claimed {
                if hooks.claim(peer.id) { claimed = true } else { try? await channel.sendFrame(.decline(id: id, reason: LinkFlow.Decline.busy)); continue }
            }
            let out = LinkFlow.receiverStep(state, flowEvent)
            state = out.state
            for f in out.send { try? await channel.sendFrame(f) }
            if let abort = out.abort { return await finish(.aborted(abort.rawValue)) }

            switch out.notify {
            case .offer:
                guard case .offered(let id, let size, let name) = state else { break }
                total = size
                offeredName = name
                timer?.cancel()
                timer = Task { [post, offerTimeoutMs] in
                    try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(offerTimeoutMs))
                    if !Task.isCancelled { post.yield(.flow(.timeout)) }
                }
                hooks.offered(LinkOffer(transferId: LinkBytes.hex(id), name: name, size: size, peerId: peer.id, peerName: peer.name))
            case .expired: return await finish(.expired)
            case .withdrawn: return await finish(.withdrawn)
            case .cancelled: return await finish(.cancelled)
            case .complete:
                do { let url = try sink?.finish(); return await finish(.completed(url)) } catch { return await finish(.aborted("disk")) }
            default: break
            }

            // Effects of the new state.
            switch (flowEvent, state) {
            case (.user(.accept), .receiving):
                timer?.cancel()
                // Only now is anything created on disk. A file that cannot be created ends the transfer with a cancel the other side sees.
                let s = LinkFileSink(folder: folder, name: offeredName)
                do { _ = try s.open(); sink = s } catch { try? await channel.sendFrame(.cancel(id: currentId(state))); return await finish(.aborted("disk")) }
            case (.frame(.chunk(_, _, let data)), .receiving(_, _, let got)):
                do { try sink?.write(data) } catch { try? await channel.sendFrame(.cancel(id: currentId(state))); return await finish(.aborted("disk")) }
                hooks.progress(got, total)
            case (.user(.decline), .idle):
                return await finish(.declined(reason: LinkFlow.Decline.user))
            default: break
            }
        }
        return await finish(.aborted("closed"))
    }

    private func currentId(_ s: LinkFlow.ReceiverState) -> Data {
        switch s { case .offered(let id, _, _), .receiving(let id, _, _): return id; case .idle: return Data(count: LinkWire.idLen) }
    }
}

// MARK: - Sending

actor LinkTransferSend {
    struct Hooks: Sendable {
        var progress: @Sendable (_ sent: UInt64, _ total: UInt64) -> Void
    }

    private enum Event: Sendable { case frame(LinkWire.Frame), closed, cancel, pumped(Bool) }

    private let transport: any LinkTransport
    private let identity: LinkIdentity
    private let peer: LinkPeer
    private let source: LinkFileSource
    private let hooks: Hooks
    private let events: AsyncStream<Event>
    private let post: AsyncStream<Event>.Continuation

    init(transport: any LinkTransport, identity: LinkIdentity, peer: LinkPeer, source: LinkFileSource, hooks: Hooks) {
        self.transport = transport
        self.identity = identity
        self.peer = peer
        self.source = source
        self.hooks = hooks
        (events, post) = AsyncStream<Event>.makeStream()
    }

    nonisolated func cancel() { post.yield(.cancel) }

    func run() async -> LinkTransferOutcome {
        func finish(_ o: LinkTransferOutcome) async -> LinkTransferOutcome { self.source.close(); await transport.close(); return o }
        let channel: LinkChannel
        do {
            guard let remote = LinkBytes.data(hex: peer.key), let me = LinkBytes.data(hex: identity.deviceId) else { return await finish(.aborted("handshake")) }
            var hs = try LinkHandshake(pattern: .kk, initiator: true, s: identity.key, rs: remote)
            try await transport.send(LinkWire.encodePreamble(mode: .transfer, deviceId: me))
            try await LinkHandshakeIO.send(transport, try hs.write(Data()))
            let reply = try hs.read(try await LinkHandshakeIO.receive(transport))
            guard reply.isEmpty else { return await finish(.aborted("handshake")) }
            let (_, send, receive) = try hs.finish()
            channel = LinkChannel(transport: transport, send: send, receive: receive)
        } catch { return await finish(.aborted("handshake")) }

        let id = Data((0..<LinkWire.idLen).map { _ in UInt8.random(in: 0...255) })
        do { try await channel.sendFrame(.offer(id: id, size: source.size, name: source.name, kind: source.kind)) } catch { return await finish(.aborted("closed")) }

        let post = self.post, file = self.source, hooks = self.hooks
        let reader = Task { do { while true { post.yield(.frame(try await channel.receiveFrame())) } } catch { post.yield(.closed) } }
        var pump: Task<Void, Never>?
        defer { reader.cancel(); pump?.cancel() }

        var state = LinkFlow.SenderState.offered(id: id)
        for await event in events {
            let flowEvent: LinkFlow.SenderEvent
            switch event {
            case .closed: return await finish(.aborted("closed"))
            case .cancel: flowEvent = .userCancel
            case .frame(let f): flowEvent = .frame(f)
            case .pumped(let ok):
                if ok { return await finish(.completed(nil)) }
                try? await channel.sendFrame(.cancel(id: id))
                return await finish(.aborted("disk"))
            }
            var declineReason: UInt8 = 0
            if case .frame(.decline(_, let r)) = flowEvent { declineReason = r }
            let out = LinkFlow.senderStep(state, flowEvent)
            let wasOffered: Bool = { if case .offered = state { return true }; return false }()
            state = out.state
            for f in out.send { try? await channel.sendFrame(f) }
            if let abort = out.abort { return await finish(.aborted(abort.rawValue)) }
            switch out.notify {
            case .declined: return await finish(.declined(reason: declineReason))
            case .cancelled: return await finish(wasOffered && flowEvent == .userCancel ? .withdrawn : .cancelled)
            default: break
            }
            if wasOffered, case .sending = state {
                // Accepted: cut the file into chunks and send them, one at a time, each waiting for the transport. A file that is shorter or longer than it was when the offer went out is cancelled.
                pump = Task {
                    var sent: UInt64 = 0
                    do {
                        for piece in LinkWire.planChunks(size: file.size) {
                            try Task.checkCancellation()
                            let data = try file.read(piece.length)
                            guard data.count == piece.length else { post.yield(.pumped(false)); return }
                            try await channel.sendFrame(.chunk(id: id, offset: piece.offset, data: data))
                            sent += UInt64(data.count)
                            hooks.progress(sent, file.size)
                        }
                        guard try file.read(1).isEmpty else { post.yield(.pumped(false)); return }
                        try await channel.sendFrame(.done(id: id))
                        post.yield(.pumped(true))
                    } catch { if !Task.isCancelled { post.yield(.pumped(false)) } }
                }
            }
        }
        return await finish(.aborted("closed"))
    }
}
````

### Task 7: The network: transport, listener with its advert, browser

**Agent:** `nm-implementer-deep` (Sonnet 5.5, high; **trigger 1**: `Network.framework` connection state callbacks, a listener with a Bonjour service and a browser, bridged into async code under Swift 6).

**Files:**
- Create: `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkNetwork.swift`, `tests/LinkNetworkTests.swift`, `scripts/test-link-network.sh`
- Modify: `NotchBuddy/NotchBuddy.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` (regenerated)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6 (`LinkTransport`, `LinkSessionError`, `LinkPairRun`), `LinkWire.encodeRecord`/`splitRecords`/`parseTxt`.
- Produces: `LinkNWTransport` (`ready(_:on:timeout:)`, `send`, `receive`, `close`, `abort`, `endpoint`), `LinkPendingSlot` (`finish()`, `isFinished`), `LinkListener(onConnection:onState:)` (`start(advert:port:)`, `stop()`, `maxPending` 8, `handshakeSeconds` 10, `serviceType`), `LinkBrowser(ownId:onResults:)` (`start()`, `stop()`, `Found {advert, endpoint}`).

**Discovery, as the spec says it.** The listener announces `_notchmeow._tcp` with a TXT record of `v`, `id` and `name` and nothing else; it **exists only while Visible is on** (that is `LinkService`'s rule, Task 8). The browser runs only while Send or Devices is open, and lists an advert only if `LinkWire.parseTxt` accepts it. **Hardening the contract does not need but a LAN listener does:** a connection that has not finished its first record and its handshake in 10 s is cancelled (`LinkPendingSlot` and its watchdog; the conversations call `handshakeDone`), at most 8 connections wait for that at once, a connection that is not `ready` in 10 s is dropped, a `.waiting` connection (no route, no permission) fails at once instead of waiting for ever, and a record of length 0 closes the connection. `close()` finishes the stream (`.finalMessage`) before it cancels, so the last frame is not cut off.

**Tests use the loopback address only.** The listener is started without an advert, so there is no Bonjour and no Local Network prompt; nothing leaves the machine. The browser and the advert are exercised by the owner's real-build check (Task 15) and the Debug fake peer (Task 12).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test and its script**

The program sends three records (the largest, 65535 bytes) through real TCP and reads them whole and in order, refuses a record of 65536 bytes before it is sent, reads a record of length 0 as a violation, runs a whole pairing through a `LinkListener` on 127.0.0.1, and checks that a connection that says nothing is cut after the 10 s deadline (the slowest part, about 10 s).

**Create `tests/LinkNetworkTests.swift`**

````swift
import Foundation
@preconcurrency import Network

// The Network.framework transport (LinkNetwork.swift) over real TCP on the loopback address: records cut out of a stream, a pairing and a transfer end to end through a LinkListener with no
// advert (so no Bonjour and no local-network prompt), a connection that stalls is cut, a record of length 0 closes. 127.0.0.1 only: nothing leaves the machine.
// Run it through scripts/test-link-network.sh.

actor Slot<T: Sendable> { var v: T?; func set(_ x: T) { v = x }; func get() -> T? { v }; var isSet: Bool { v != nil } }

@main
enum LinkNetworkTests {
    static func identity(_ n: UInt8, _ name: String) -> LinkIdentity {
        LinkIdentity(deviceId: String(repeating: String(format: "%02x", n), count: 16), name: name, key: try! LinkNoise.privateKey(raw: Data(repeating: n, count: 32)))
    }

    /// Starts a listener on a free loopback port and waits for its port.
    static func listen(_ onConnection: @escaping @Sendable (LinkNWTransport, LinkPendingSlot) -> Void) async -> (LinkListener, UInt16) {
        let port = Slot<UInt16>()
        let l = LinkListener(onConnection: onConnection, onState: { p, _ in if let p { Task { await port.set(p) } } })
        try! l.start(advert: nil)
        for _ in 0..<400 { if await port.isSet { break }; try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(5)) }
        return (l, await port.get() ?? 0)
    }

    static func connect(_ port: UInt16) async throws -> LinkNWTransport {
        let c = NWConnection(host: "127.0.0.1", port: NWEndpoint.Port(rawValue: port)!, using: .tcp)
        return try await LinkNWTransport.ready(c, on: DispatchQueue(label: "test.client"))
    }

    @MainActor static func main() async {
        setvbuf(stdout, nil, _IONBF, 0)
        DispatchQueue.global().asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 90) { print("timed out"); exit(1) }

        CT.section("records over TCP")
        let server = Slot<LinkNWTransport>()
        let (listener, port) = await listen { t, slot in slot.finish(); Task { await server.set(t) } }
        CT.checkTrue("the listener has a port", port != 0)
        let client = try! await connect(port)
        for _ in 0..<400 { if await server.isSet { break }; try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(5)) }
        let s = await server.get()!
        try! await client.send(Data("one".utf8))
        try! await client.send(Data(count: 65535))
        try! await client.send(Data("three".utf8))
        let r1 = try! await s.receive(), r2 = try! await s.receive(), r3 = try! await s.receive()
        CT.check("three records arrive whole and in order, the big one too", [String(decoding: r1, as: UTF8.self), "\(r2.count)", String(decoding: r3, as: UTF8.self)], ["one", "65535", "three"])
        var tooLong = false
        do { try await client.send(Data(count: 65536)) } catch { tooLong = (error as? LinkSessionError) == .violation("record length") }
        CT.checkTrue("a record of 65536 bytes is refused before it is sent", tooLong)
        try! await s.send(Data("back".utf8))
        CT.check("and the other way", String(decoding: try! await client.receive(), as: UTF8.self), "back")
        await client.close()
        var closed = false
        do { _ = try await s.receive() } catch { closed = (error as? LinkSessionError) == .closed }
        CT.checkTrue("a closed connection ends the other side's read", closed)

        CT.section("a record of length 0 closes")
        let c2 = NWConnection(host: "127.0.0.1", port: NWEndpoint.Port(rawValue: port)!, using: .tcp)
        let raw = try! await LinkNWTransport.ready(c2, on: DispatchQueue(label: "test.raw"))
        for _ in 0..<400 { if let t = await server.get(), t !== s { break }; try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(5)) }
        let s2 = await server.get()!
        c2.send(content: Data([0, 0]), completion: .contentProcessed { _ in })
        var violation = false
        do { _ = try await s2.receive() } catch { violation = (error as? LinkSessionError) == .violation("record") }
        CT.checkTrue("the reader throws a violation", violation)
        await raw.close()

        CT.section("a pairing through the listener")
        listener.stop()
        let mac = identity(0x11, "Studio Mac"), pc = identity(0x22, "Desk PC")
        let pcPeer = LinkPeer(id: pc.deviceId, name: pc.name, key: LinkBytes.hex(LinkNoise.publicKey(of: pc.key)), at: 1), macPeer = LinkPeer(id: mac.deviceId, name: mac.name, key: LinkBytes.hex(LinkNoise.publicKey(of: mac.key)), at: 1)
        let outcomes = Slot<LinkPairOutcome>(), codes = Slot<String>(), codesA = Slot<String>()
        let responderRun = Slot<LinkPairRun>()
        let (l2, port2) = await listen { t, slot in
            Task {
                guard let pre = try? LinkWire.decodePreamble(try await t.receive()), pre.mode == .pair else { slot.finish(); await t.close(); return }
                let run = LinkPairRun(transport: t, identity: pc, nonce: Data(repeating: 0xB1, count: 32), clock: { Int(DispatchTime.now().uptimeNanoseconds / 1_000_000) }, hooks: .init(showCode: { c, _ in Task { await codes.set(c) } }, handshakeDone: { slot.finish() }))
                await responderRun.set(run)
                let (o, _) = await run.runResponder(.init())
                await outcomes.set(o)
            }
        }
        let t = try! await connect(port2)
        let initiator = LinkPairRun(transport: t, identity: mac, nonce: Data(repeating: 0xA1, count: 32), clock: { Int(DispatchTime.now().uptimeNanoseconds / 1_000_000) }, hooks: .init(showCode: { c, _ in Task { await codesA.set(c) } }))
        Task {
            for _ in 0..<400 { if await codes.isSet, await codesA.isSet { break }; try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(5)) }
            initiator.answer(true); await responderRun.get()?.answer(true)
        }
        let (oi, _) = await initiator.runInitiator(.init())
        for _ in 0..<400 { if await outcomes.isSet { break }; try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(5)) }
        CT.check("both sides are paired over a real socket, and saw the same code", [oi == .paired(pcPeer.withAt(oi)), await outcomes.get() == .paired(macPeer.withAt(await outcomes.get())), await codes.get() == codesA.get()], [true, true, true])
        l2.stop()

        CT.section("a connection that says nothing is cut")
        let cut = Slot<Bool>()
        let (l3, port3) = await listen { t, slot in
            Task {
                do { _ = try await t.receive() } catch { await cut.set(true) }
                slot.finish()
            }
        }
        let quiet = NWConnection(host: "127.0.0.1", port: NWEndpoint.Port(rawValue: port3)!, using: .tcp)
        _ = try! await LinkNWTransport.ready(quiet, on: DispatchQueue(label: "test.quiet"))
        for _ in 0..<(LinkListener.handshakeSeconds * 200 + 400) { if await cut.isSet { break }; try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(5)) }
        CT.checkTrue("after \(LinkListener.handshakeSeconds) seconds of silence the listener cancels the connection", await cut.isSet)
        quiet.cancel(); l3.stop()
        CT.finish()
    }
}

extension LinkPeer {
    /// The same peer with the time of whatever outcome is compared (the pairing stamps the time it ended).
    func withAt(_ o: LinkPairOutcome?) -> LinkPeer { if case .paired(let p)? = o { return LinkPeer(id: id, name: name, key: key, at: p.at) }; return self }
}
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the implementation**

**Create `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkNetwork.swift`**

````swift
@preconcurrency import Network
import Foundation

// The link on the network (sub-project G2): a transport over a TCP connection (`Network.framework`), the listener that advertises `_notchmeow._tcp`, and the browser that finds the other machines.
// Everything here is event-driven: the listener and the browser are callbacks on a private queue, the connection reads wait on the socket, and **nothing polls**. There is no listener and no
// advert until `LinkService` is told the Mac is visible on the network (Settings → Devices, off by default), and the browser runs only while Send or Devices is open.
//
// Hardening that the contract does not need but a listener on a LAN does: a connection that has not finished its first record and its handshake in 10 seconds is cancelled, and no more than 8
// connections wait at once for that (`LinkListener.maxPending`); records are cut by `LinkWire.splitRecords` (a length of 0 closes, a record is at most 65535 bytes, so the buffer stays small).

/// A TCP connection as a `LinkTransport`: the bytes of the stream are cut into whole records on the way in and given a length on the way out (`LinkWire`).
actor LinkNWTransport: LinkTransport {
    private let connection: NWConnection
    private var buffer = Data()
    private var queue: [Data] = []
    private var closed = false

    init(_ connection: NWConnection) { self.connection = connection }

    /// Starts an accepted or new connection and waits until it is ready (or has failed). `timeout` bounds the wait.
    static func ready(_ connection: NWConnection, on queue: DispatchQueue, timeout: Duration = .seconds(10)) async throws -> LinkNWTransport {
        try await withThrowingTaskGroup(of: Void.self) { group in
            group.addTask {
                try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { (c: CheckedContinuation<Void, Error>) in
                    let once = Once()
                    connection.stateUpdateHandler = { state in
                        switch state {
                        case .ready: if once.take() { c.resume() }
                        case .failed(let e): if once.take() { c.resume(throwing: LinkSessionError.io("\(e)")) }
                        case .cancelled: if once.take() { c.resume(throwing: LinkSessionError.closed) }
                        case .waiting(let e): if once.take() { c.resume(throwing: LinkSessionError.io("waiting: \(e)")) }   // no route, no permission: fail at once, do not wait for ever
                        default: break
                        }
                    }
                    connection.start(queue: queue)
                }
            }
            group.addTask { try await Task.sleep(for: timeout); throw LinkSessionError.io("connect timeout") }
            defer { group.cancelAll() }
            do { try await group.next() } catch { connection.cancel(); throw error }
            connection.stateUpdateHandler = nil
            return LinkNWTransport(connection)
        }
    }

    func send(_ record: Data) async throws {
        guard !closed else { throw LinkSessionError.closed }
        let framed: Data
        do { framed = try LinkWire.encodeRecord(record) } catch { throw LinkSessionError.violation("record length") }
        try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { (c: CheckedContinuation<Void, Error>) in
            connection.send(content: framed, completion: .contentProcessed { error in
                if let error { c.resume(throwing: LinkSessionError.io("\(error)")) } else { c.resume() }
            })
        }
    }

    func receive() async throws -> Data {
        while true {
            if !queue.isEmpty { return queue.removeFirst() }
            if closed { throw LinkSessionError.closed }
            let chunk: Data? = await withCheckedContinuation { (c: CheckedContinuation<Data?, Never>) in
                connection.receive(minimumIncompleteLength: 1, maximumLength: 65_537) { data, _, isComplete, error in
                    if let data, !data.isEmpty { c.resume(returning: data) } else { c.resume(returning: nil); _ = (isComplete, error) }
                }
            }
            guard let chunk else { closed = true; throw LinkSessionError.closed }
            buffer.append(chunk)
            do {
                let (records, rest) = try LinkWire.splitRecords(buffer)
                buffer = rest
                queue.append(contentsOf: records)
            } catch { closed = true; connection.cancel(); throw LinkSessionError.violation("record") }   // a record of length 0
        }
    }

    /// Sends what is queued, tells the other side the stream is finished, and cancels once that went out.
    func close() async {
        guard !closed else { return }
        closed = true
        await withCheckedContinuation { (c: CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>) in
            connection.send(content: nil, contentContext: .finalMessage, isComplete: true, completion: .contentProcessed { _ in c.resume() })
        }
        connection.cancel()
    }

    /// The other end's address, for the log-free "who is this" of a connection (never logged, never shown).
    nonisolated var endpoint: NWEndpoint { connection.endpoint }

    /// Hard stop: for a connection that is too slow. The pending reads then fail and the conversation ends.
    nonisolated func abort() { connection.cancel() }

    private final class Once: @unchecked Sendable {
        private let lock = NSLock(); private var taken = false
        func take() -> Bool { lock.withLock { if taken { return false }; taken = true; return true } }
    }
}

/// A connection that has been accepted but has not finished its handshake. The service calls `finish()` when it has (or when it refuses the connection); until then a watchdog cuts the connection
/// after `LinkListener.handshakeSeconds`, and the slot counts against `LinkListener.maxPending`.
final class LinkPendingSlot: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var done = false
    private var release: (@Sendable () -> Void)?
    private var watchdog: Task<Void, Never>?

    fileprivate init(release: @escaping @Sendable () -> Void) { self.release = release }
    fileprivate func arm(_ task: Task<Void, Never>) { lock.withLock { if done { task.cancel() } else { watchdog = task } } }
    var isFinished: Bool { lock.withLock { done } }

    func finish() {
        let (r, w): ((@Sendable () -> Void)?, Task<Void, Never>?) = lock.withLock {
            if done { return (nil, nil) }
            done = true
            defer { release = nil; watchdog = nil }
            return (release, watchdog)
        }
        w?.cancel()
        r?()
    }
}

/// The listener: accepts connections, advertises the machine, and hands each new connection to the service. It exists only while the machine is visible on the network.
final class LinkListener: @unchecked Sendable {
    static let serviceType = "_notchmeow._tcp"
    static let maxPending = 8
    static let handshakeSeconds = 10

    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "notchmeow.link.listener")
    private var listener: NWListener?
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var pending = 0
    private let onConnection: @Sendable (LinkNWTransport, LinkPendingSlot) -> Void
    private let onState: @Sendable (_ port: UInt16?, _ failed: Bool) -> Void

    /// `onConnection` gets a connection that is ready, with its pending slot. `onState` says when the listener is ready (with its port) or has failed.
    init(onConnection: @escaping @Sendable (LinkNWTransport, LinkPendingSlot) -> Void, onState: @escaping @Sendable (_ port: UInt16?, _ failed: Bool) -> Void) {
        self.onConnection = onConnection
        self.onState = onState
    }

    /// Starts listening. With `advert` the machine is announced as `_notchmeow._tcp` (a TXT record with `v`, `id` and `name`, nothing else); without it (the tests and the loopback of the Debug fake
    /// peer) only the port exists. `port` 0 lets the system choose.
    func start(advert: (id: String, name: String)?, port: UInt16 = 0) throws {
        let params = NWParameters.tcp
        params.includePeerToPeer = false
        let l = try NWListener(using: params, on: port == 0 ? .any : NWEndpoint.Port(rawValue: port)!)
        if let advert {
            l.service = NWListener.Service(name: String(advert.id.prefix(12)), type: Self.serviceType, domain: nil, txtRecord: NWTXTRecord(["v": String(LinkWire.protocolVersion), "id": advert.id, "name": advert.name]))
        }
        l.stateUpdateHandler = { [weak self] state in
            guard let self else { return }
            switch state {
            case .ready: self.onState(l.port?.rawValue, false)
            case .failed, .waiting: self.onState(nil, true)
            default: break
            }
        }
        l.newConnectionHandler = { [weak self] conn in self?.accept(conn) }
        listener = l
        l.start(queue: queue)
    }

    func stop() {
        listener?.cancel()
        listener = nil
    }

    private func accept(_ conn: NWConnection) {
        let admitted: Bool = lock.withLock { if pending >= Self.maxPending { return false }; pending += 1; return true }
        guard admitted else { conn.cancel(); return }
        let slot = LinkPendingSlot { [weak self] in self?.lock.withLock { self?.pending -= 1 } }
        let q = queue, handler = onConnection
        Task {
            do {
                let transport = try await LinkNWTransport.ready(conn, on: q, timeout: .seconds(Self.handshakeSeconds))
                slot.arm(Task { try? await Task.sleep(for: .seconds(Self.handshakeSeconds)); if !Task.isCancelled && !slot.isFinished { transport.abort(); slot.finish() } })
                handler(transport, slot)
            } catch { conn.cancel(); slot.finish() }
        }
    }
}

/// The browser: finds the other machines' adverts. It runs only while Send or Devices is open (`LinkService` counts the openers).
final class LinkBrowser: @unchecked Sendable {
    struct Found: Sendable { let advert: LinkWire.Advert; let endpoint: NWEndpoint }

    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "notchmeow.link.browser")
    private var browser: NWBrowser?
    private let ownId: String
    private let onResults: @Sendable ([Found]) -> Void

    init(ownId: String, onResults: @escaping @Sendable ([Found]) -> Void) {
        self.ownId = ownId
        self.onResults = onResults
    }

    func start() {
        guard browser == nil else { return }
        let b = NWBrowser(for: .bonjourWithTXTRecord(type: LinkListener.serviceType, domain: nil), using: .tcp)
        b.browseResultsChangedHandler = { [ownId, onResults] results, _ in
            var found: [Found] = []
            for r in results {
                guard case .bonjour(let txt) = r.metadata else { continue }
                let dict = txt.dictionary
                // An advert that does not pass the contract's checks (version, id, name, this machine's own id) is not listed.
                if let ad = try? LinkWire.parseTxt(dict, ownId: .value(ownId)) { found.append(Found(advert: ad, endpoint: r.endpoint)) }
            }
            onResults(found)
        }
        browser = b
        b.start(queue: queue)
    }

    func stop() {
        browser?.cancel()
        browser = nil
    }
}
````

### Task 8: The service, the `.link` view, the cards and Settings → Devices

**Agent:** `nm-implementer-deep` (Sonnet 5.5, high; **trigger 1**: a `@MainActor` service that joins actors, sockets, the Keychain, the island's window controller and the Settings window; many cross-task interfaces, so trigger 5's care applies too).

**Files:**
- Create: `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/LinkService.swift`, `LinkViews.swift`; `scripts/render-link.sh`, `scripts/render-link.swift`
- Modify: `NotchBuddy/Sources/App/AppState.swift`, `IslandTypes.swift`, `IslandViewContent.swift`, `IslandWindowController.swift`, `SettingsView.swift`, `AppDelegate.swift`; `tests/ShelfWiringTests.swift`; `NotchBuddy/NotchBuddy.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` (regenerated)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1 to 7; G1's `ShelfModel.open`, `ShelfItem`; the island's `CardBackground`, `PrimaryButton`, `SecondaryButton`, `CodeBlock`; `appendAppLog`; `Notification.Name.hookExpand`/`.islandCollapse`/`.openFullSettings`.
- Produces: `LinkService.shared` (`settings`, `rows`, `listening`, `problem`, `card`, `sendStatus`, `note`, `start()`, `setVisible(_:)`, `setDeviceName(_:)`, `beginBrowsing()`, `endBrowsing()`, `pair(with:)`, `answerPair(yes:)`, `forget(_:)`, `devicesOpened()`, `devicesClosed()`, `respondOffer(accept:)`, `cancelReceiving()`, `dismissCard()`, `send(_:to:model:)`, `cancelSending()`, `nowMs()`, `marker`, `Card`), `LinkText`, `LinkCardActions`, `LinkCardView`, `DevicesSettingsView`, `DevicesSettingsSection`, `LinkCardHost`, `IslandView.link`, `AppState.linkOfferPending`/`linkHoldsIsland`.

**What the service does, and will not do.** It starts **no listener and makes no advert until the user turns Visible on** (`start()` starts one only `if settings.visible`; Visible off stops the listener and cancels what is running). It browses only between `beginBrowsing()` and `endBrowsing()` (Devices and, in Task 11, the shelf); `DevicesSettingsSection` also tells it when Devices is on screen (`devicesOpened()`/`devicesClosed()`), which is what opens the pairing gate. An incoming connection is read for its preamble: a pairing is run only through `LinkIncomingPairing.run`, behind the gate (**only while Settings → Devices is open on this Mac, or within 2 minutes after the user clicked Pair here; otherwise refused silently, nothing sent, no card, no alert**; one at a time), a transfer is run only for a device id this Mac has paired (anything else is closed without an answer), and an offer becomes `.offer` on the `.link` card, which **only a click** answers (`respondOffer(accept:)`). On `.paired` the key is stored with `LinkKeychain.add`, which reads the paired map **again at that moment**. It logs only fixed sentences. The `.link` view is shown by the existing `hookExpand` notification and holds the island open through `AppState.linkHoldsIsland` while a pairing, an offer or a file coming in is on it.

**The views.** `LinkCardView` draws the pairing card ("Pair with <name>?", the six digits in two groups, "They match" and "No"), the Accept card (the **sanitized** name and size in a `CodeBlock`, "Decline" and "Accept", a note that the file is saved in Downloads/NotchMeow and never opened, and declined by itself after 2 minutes), the progress of a file coming in (a drawn bar, not a `ProgressView`: an AppKit-backed control cannot be drawn into the render script's pictures) with "Cancel", and the end of any of them ("Show in Finder", "OK"). `DevicesSettingsView` is Settings' own `GroupBox` rows: "This Mac" (the Visible switch, the name, a status line), "Machines" (paired online, paired not seen, available; Pair and Forget with a second click; the note; the Local Network permission button and its help line) and "Received files". Both take plain values and closures so `render-link.sh` draws them without the app.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the render script**

It compiles every file of the app but `NotchBuddyApp.swift` (the cards use the island's `CardBackground` and buttons; about two minutes), runs in a home folder of its own through `render-home-guard.sh`, and draws `--cards`, `--devices` (through a hosting view: `ImageRenderer` cannot draw a `GroupBox`) and `--shelf`.

**Create `scripts/render-link.swift`**

````swift
import AppKit
import SwiftUI

// Draws the link's pictures (sub-project G2) without launching NotchMeow: the app's own SwiftUI views (LinkViews.swift, and the shelf bar's "To a device" button) go through ImageRenderer into PNGs.
// Run it through scripts/render-link.sh:  bash scripts/render-link.sh --cards --out DIR   |   --devices --out DIR   |   --shelf --out DIR
// LinkViews.swift uses the island's CardBackground and buttons (IslandViewContent.swift), so the .sh compiles every file of the app but NotchBuddyApp.swift, as render-approval-card.sh does.

enum Shot {
    static let scale: CGFloat = 2

    /// A view with AppKit-backed controls (GroupBox, Toggle, TextField, Button): drawn through a hosting view, which ImageRenderer cannot do.
    @MainActor static func saveHosted<V: View>(_ view: V, size: CGSize, to url: URL) {
        let host = NSHostingView(rootView: view)
        host.frame = NSRect(origin: .zero, size: size)
        let win = NSWindow(contentRect: host.frame, styleMask: [.borderless], backing: .buffered, defer: false)
        win.appearance = NSAppearance(named: .darkAqua)
        win.contentView = host
        host.layoutSubtreeIfNeeded()
        guard let rep = host.bitmapImageRepForCachingDisplay(in: host.bounds) else { exit(1) }
        host.cacheDisplay(in: host.bounds, to: rep)
        try! rep.representation(using: .png, properties: [:])!.write(to: url)
        print("wrote \(url.path)")
    }

    @MainActor static func save<V: View>(_ view: V, to url: URL) {
        let r = ImageRenderer(content: view)
        r.scale = scale
        r.isOpaque = true
        guard let cg = r.cgImage, let png = NSBitmapImageRep(cgImage: cg).representation(using: .png, properties: [:]) else {
            FileHandle.standardError.write(Data("could not draw \(url.lastPathComponent)\n".utf8)); exit(1)
        }
        try! png.write(to: url)
        print("wrote \(url.path)")
    }
}

/// The island around a card, as the island puts it: black, the card 10 pt in and 42 down, 98 high, and a round stand-in where the cat is (the cat needs the engine; the card is what is under test).
struct IslandAround<Content: View>: View {
    let caption: String
    let content: Content
    init(_ caption: String, @ViewBuilder content: () -> Content) { self.caption = caption; self.content = content() }
    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
            Text(caption).font(.system(size: 11, weight: .semibold)).foregroundColor(.white.opacity(0.85))
            ZStack(alignment: .topLeading) {
                Color.black
                content.frame(width: 620, height: 98).offset(x: 10, y: 42)
                Circle().fill(Color(hex: "#C9A27E")).frame(width: 56, height: 56).offset(x: 62 - 28, y: 96 - 28)
            }
            .frame(width: 640, height: 160, alignment: .topLeading)
            .foregroundColor(Color(hex: "#F5F6F8"))
            .clipShape(UnevenRoundedRectangle(bottomLeadingRadius: IslandConst.expandedCorner, bottomTrailingRadius: IslandConst.expandedCorner))
        }
    }
}

struct CardsSheet: View {
    static let offer = LinkOffer(transferId: String(repeating: "a1", count: 16), name: "Quarterly report.pdf", size: 2_400_000, peerId: String(repeating: "e1", count: 16), peerName: "Desk PC")
    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            IslandAround("Pairing: this Mac and Desk PC show the same six digits") { LinkCardView(card: .pair(code: "483291", peer: "Desk PC", answered: false)) }
            IslandAround("Pairing: this user answered, waiting for the other machine") { LinkCardView(card: .pair(code: "483291", peer: "Desk PC", answered: true)) }
            IslandAround("Accept card: nothing happens until a click") { LinkCardView(card: .offer(Self.offer)) }
            IslandAround("Receiving") { LinkCardView(card: .receiving(Self.offer, received: 1_080_000)) }
            IslandAround("Done") { LinkCardView(card: .result(title: "Received", detail: "Saved as Quarterly report.pdf in Downloads/NotchMeow.", file: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/tmp/x"))) }
            IslandAround("The other side stopped") { LinkCardView(card: .result(title: "Transfer stopped", detail: "The connection ended before the file was complete. Nothing was saved.", file: nil)) }
        }
        .padding(16).frame(width: 672, alignment: .topLeading).background(Color(red: 0.30, green: 0.31, blue: 0.34))
    }
}

struct DevicesSheet: View {
    static let rows: [LinkDirectory.Row] = [.init(id: "1", name: "Desk PC", state: .pairedOnline), .init(id: "2", name: "Old laptop", state: .pairedOffline), .init(id: "3", name: "Studio Mac", state: .available)]
    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 24) {
            Text("Off (the default): nothing listens, nothing is announced").font(.system(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
            DevicesSettingsView(visible: false, deviceName: "Sam's MacBook Pro", listening: false, problem: nil, note: nil, rows: [])
            Divider()
            Text("On, with machines").font(.system(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
            DevicesSettingsView(visible: true, deviceName: "Sam's MacBook Pro", listening: true, problem: nil, note: "Wait 30 seconds before trying again.", rows: Self.rows)
            Divider()
            Text("On, nothing found, and the listener could not start").font(.system(size: 12, weight: .semibold))
            DevicesSettingsView(visible: true, deviceName: "Sam's MacBook Pro", listening: false, problem: "This Mac could not listen. Check System Settings, Privacy & Security, Local Network.", note: nil, rows: [])
        }
        .padding(20).frame(width: 640, alignment: .topLeading).background(Color(nsColor: .windowBackgroundColor)).environment(\.colorScheme, .dark)
    }
}

@MainActor
struct ShelfBarSheet: View {
    var body: some View {
        let dir = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("render-link-\(UUID().uuidString)", isDirectory: true)
        let _ = try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: dir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let file = dir.appendingPathComponent("Quarterly report.pdf"); let _ = try? Data(count: 2_400_000).write(to: file)
        let model = ShelfModel(store: ShelfStore(support: dir.appendingPathComponent("support"), scoped: false))
        let _ = model.add([.file(file)])
        let _ = model.toggle(model.items[0].id)
        let buttons = ShelfButtons(ask: { _ in }, show: { _ in }, send: { _ in }, airDrop: { _ in }, showDevicesButton: true,
                                   devices: [ShelfDeviceChoice(id: "1", name: "Desk PC", online: true), ShelfDeviceChoice(id: "2", name: "Old laptop", online: false)], deviceLine: "Sending Quarterly report.pdf to Desk PC, 45 %")
        ZStack(alignment: .topLeading) {
            RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 22).fill(Color.black).frame(width: 640, height: 176)
            RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 20).fill(Color(hex: "#141518")).frame(width: 620, height: 124).offset(x: 10, y: 42)
            ShelfContent(model: model, picture: true, buttons: buttons).frame(width: 620, height: 124, alignment: .leading).offset(x: 10, y: 42)
        }.frame(width: 640, height: 176, alignment: .topLeading).padding(16).background(Color(red: 0.30, green: 0.31, blue: 0.34))
    }
}

@main
struct RenderLink {
    @MainActor static func main() {
        var out = ".", cards = false, devices = false, shelf = false
        var it = CommandLine.arguments.dropFirst().makeIterator()
        while let k = it.next() {
            switch k {
            case "--out": out = it.next() ?? out
            case "--cards": cards = true
            case "--devices": devices = true
            case "--shelf": shelf = true
            default: FileHandle.standardError.write(Data("unknown argument \(k)\n".utf8)); exit(2)
            }
        }
        _ = NSApplication.shared
        let dir = URL(fileURLWithPath: out, isDirectory: true)
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: dir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        if cards { Shot.save(CardsSheet(), to: dir.appendingPathComponent("link-cards.png")) }
        if devices { Shot.saveHosted(DevicesSheet(), size: CGSize(width: 640, height: 1180), to: dir.appendingPathComponent("link-devices.png")) }
        if shelf { Shot.save(ShelfBarSheet(), to: dir.appendingPathComponent("link-shelf-bar.png")) }
        if !cards && !devices && !shelf { print("usage: render-link.sh --cards|--devices|--shelf --out DIR"); exit(2) }
    }
}
````

**Create `scripts/render-link.sh`**

````bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Draws the link's pictures (sub-project G2) without launching NotchMeow (see scripts/render-link.swift).
#   bash scripts/render-link.sh --cards --out DIR     the pairing card, the Accept card, the progress and the end of a transfer, each in the island
#   bash scripts/render-link.sh --devices --out DIR   Settings → Devices: off, on with machines, and the problem notes
#   bash scripts/render-link.sh --shelf --out DIR     the shelf's bar with the "To a device" button and the status of a send
# The cards use the island's CardBackground and buttons, which sit in IslandViewContent.swift, so this compiles every file of the app but NotchBuddyApp.swift (about two minutes), as
# render-approval-card.sh does, and runs in a home folder of its own (scripts/render-home-guard.sh kills it if it opens the real ~/.claude, ~/.gemini or ~/.codex). Nothing is launched or listened to.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
BUILD_DIR="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/notchmeow-render-link.XXXXXX")"
REAL_HOME="$HOME"
cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$BUILD_DIR"
    defaults delete render-link > /dev/null 2>&1 || true
    rm -f "$REAL_HOME/Library/Preferences/render-link.plist"
}
trap cleanup EXIT
export NM_LOG_DIR="$BUILD_DIR/logs"
mkdir -p "$BUILD_DIR/home"
APP=NotchBuddy/Sources/App
FILES=()
while IFS= read -r f; do FILES+=("$f"); done < <(find "$APP" -name '*.swift' ! -name NotchBuddyApp.swift | sort)
swiftc -swift-version 6 -suppress-warnings -D DEBUG "${FILES[@]}" scripts/render-link.swift -o "$BUILD_DIR/render-link"
. scripts/render-home-guard.sh
render_home_guard "$BUILD_DIR/home" "$BUILD_DIR/render-link" "$@"
````

Run: `chmod +x scripts/render-link.sh && bash -n scripts/render-link.sh`
Expected: no output.
